%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000005445000160  
%            ALGOL ACCESS TO POSIX PROCEDURES                           0000005545000180  
%                                                                       0000005645000200  
% This ALGOL include file provides POSIX library procedure              0000005745000220  
% declarations for the POSIX MCPSUPPORT library interfaces.  This file  0000005845000240  
% can be included in ALGOL programs.  Programmers can access the new    0000005945000260  
% procedures using the names defined in this file instead of the actual 0000006045000280  
% names.  The names are chosen to mimic the "C" POSIX API calls.        0000006145000300  
% Additional symbols are defined to give more flexibility to the user   0000006245000320  
% for setting flags or values.                                          0000006345000340  
%                                                                       0000006445000360  
%  This file is organized in the following fashion:                     0000006545000380  
%                                                                       0000006645000400  
%  o  Part 1 of the file contains the interface declarations and        0000006745000420  
%     selector values                                                   0000006845000430  
%     1.   signal related calls                                         0000006945000440  
%     2.   semaphore related calls                                      0000007045000460  
%     3.   user/group info related calls                                0000007145000480  
%     4.   selector values are defined to symbols                       0000007245000490  
%                                                                       0000007345000500  
%  o  Part 2 of the file contains the macro definitions                 0000007445000520  
%     1.   errno values are defined to symbols                          0000007545000540  
%     2.   other flags are defined to symbols                           0000007645000580  
%                                                                       0000007745000600  
%  o  Part 3 of the file contains the re-definition of procedure        0000007845000620  
%     calls to simulate "C" interface call names.                       0000007945000640  
%                                                                       0000008045000660  
%  It is recommended that user access to these procedures be            0000008145000680  
%  made using the constants in PART 2 and the interfaces in PART 3.     0000008245000700  
%                                                                       0000008345000720  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000008445000740  
%                                                                       0000008545000760  
%                                                                       0000008645000780  
%  MCPSUPPORT Library Interfaces for POSIX                              0000008745000800  
%                                                                       0000008845000820  
%  General Definition Rules                                             0000008945000840  
%  ========================                                             0000009045000860  
%  1. All MCP interfaces which take pointers to character strings or    0000009145000880  
%     structures as input parameters will also take a by-value integer  0000009245000900  
%     string or structure length as a separate parameter.  The appli-   0000009345000920  
%     cation is responsible for providing this length. The MCP routines 0000009445000940  
%     will rely on this length information to determine how much of the 0000009545000960  
%     string or structure information is valid.                         0000009645000980  
%                                                                       0000009745001000  
%     In the case of strings, if the length is -1, the called function  0000009845001020  
%     will assume that the string is NULL terminated, and act according-0000009945001040  
%     ly.  If a string length is provided, it is assumed to NOT include 0000010045001060  
%     any trailing NULL.                                                0000010145001080  
%                                                                       0000010245001100  
%     All input structures with embedded strings will include an integer0000010345001120  
%     preceding the start of the string.  This integer will contain the 0000010445001140  
%     length of the string. If the length is -1, the MCP will assume    0000010545001160  
%     that the string is NULL terminated; if the length is not -1, it   0000010645001180  
%     will be taken as the length of the string,  not including the     0000010745001200  
%     NULL.                                                             0000010845001220  
%                                                                       0000010945001240  
%  2. All MCP interfaces which take pointers to character strings or    0000011045001260  
%     structures as output parameters will also return the length of the0000011145001280  
%     string or structure in a by-reference integer output parameter    0000011245001300  
%     unless such information can be returned as the interface result.  0000011345001320  
%     (See 5. for more details.)                                        0000011445001340  
%     Additionally, a by-value integer maximum string or structure      0000011545001360  
%     length input parameter must be provided.  The MCP interfaces      0000011645001380  
%     require a valid string or structure length maximum value, since   0000011745001400  
%     this length in conjunction with the actual size of the string or  0000011845001420  
%     structure container will determine how much of the string or      0000011945001440  
%     structure information will be written.                            0000012045001460  
%                                                                       0000012145001480  
%     The MCP interfaces will place a terminating NULL at the end of an 0000012245001500  
%     output character string if there is room and the returned length  0000012345001520  
%     will not include the NULL character.                              0000012445001540  
%                                                                       0000012545001560  
%     No error will be returned if there is no space for a NULL         0000012645001580  
%     character at the end of the data. However if there is insufficient0000012745001600  
%     room for the data, then EINVAL will be returned in the            0000012845001620  
%     by-reference integer output parameter ERRNO and unexpected result 0000012945001640  
%     will be returned. The documentation for each interface specifies  0000013045001660  
%     the behavior for all possible conditions.                         0000013145001700  
%                                                                       0000013245001720  
%     All output structures with embedded strings will include an       0000013345001740  
%     integer preceding the start of the string.  This integer will     0000013445001760  
%     contain the length of the string, excluding the NULL; overflow    0000013545001780  
%     conditions will be handled as described above.                    0000013645001800  
%                                                                       0000013745001820  
%  3. The application is responsible for guaranteeing the alignment of  0000013845001840  
%     embedded word numeric types (i.e.  integers) within structures for0000013945001860  
%     all MCP interfaces which take pointers to structures.  All        0000014045001880  
%     structures must start on a word boundary.                         0000014145001900  
%                                                                       0000014245001920  
%  4. For MCP interfaces with Selector parameters, any parameter (input 0000014345001940  
%     or output) is undefined if not used for that selector value. The  0000014445001960  
%     documentation for each interface states which parameters are      0000014545001980  
%     valid and which are undefined for each selector value.            0000014645002000  
%                                                                       0000014745002020  
%  5. All MCP interfaces return an integer or real result.  The result, 0000014845002040  
%     -1, indicates that an error has occurred and that ERRNO has       0000014945002060  
%     probably been modified.  A meaningful positive or negative result 0000015045002080  
%     may also be returned if specified by POSIX. MCP interfaces        0000015145002100  
%     never return a pointer result; however, if the POSIX              0000015245002120  
%     interface either returns a pointer, or an output pointer is its   0000015345002140  
%     only output parameter AND the function returns no other meaningful0000015445002160  
%     result, then the results of such functions will be the length of  0000015545002180  
%     the string supplied by the MCP.                                   0000015645002200  
%                                                                       0000015745002220  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000015845002240  
$$PAGE                                                                  0000015945003000  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                                   0000016045003020  
%    PART 1         %                                                   0000016145003040  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                                   0000016245003060

% POSIX_FCNTL CMD values    %                                           0000144545607460  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                           0000144645607480  
% CMD values and associated parameters are as follows:                  0000144745607500  
   F_DUPFD  = 0 #, % Only supported for POSIX Global Files.  Causes file0000144845607520  
                   % descriptor to be copied into the first available   0000144945607540  
                   % file descriptor vector table entry.                0000145045607560  
   F_GETFD  = 1 #, % Returns current value of the file descriptor flags.0000145145607580  
   F_SETFD  = 2 #, % Sets the file descriptor flags.                    0000145245607600  
   F_GETFL  = 3 #, % Returns current value of the file status flags.    0000145345607620  
   F_SETFL  = 4 #, % Sets the file status flags.                        0000145445607640  
   F_GETLK  = 5 #, % Get the first lock which blocks the lock described 0000145545607660  
                   % by the flock structure.                            0000145645607680  
   F_SETLK  = 6 #, % An attempt is made to set or clear the lock        0000145745607700  
                   % specified by the flock structure.                  0000145845607720  
   F_SETLKW = 7 #; %Same as F_SETLK except when a blocking lock is found0000145945607740  
                                                                        0000146045607760  
DEFINE                                                                  0000146145607780  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%    0000146245607800  
%       POSIX flock STRUCTURE (POSIX_FCNTL)                        %    0000146345607820  
%..................................................................%    0000146445607840  
% The flock structure is used to specify locks for POSIX defined   %    0000146545607860  
% advisory locking.  Advisory locking is supported by POSIX_FCNTL. %    0000146645607880  
% POSIX_FCNTL uses FILELOCKER which also provides record locking   %    0000146745607900  
% for general A Series use.  The words in the flock structure map  %    0000146845607920  
% to FILELOCKER pararmeters.                                       %    0000146945607940  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%    0000147045607960  
    %-Word-offset-- %--Description--------------  %--FILELOCKER params  0000147145607980  
    L_TYPE   = 0 #, % Type of lock.               % TYPE                0000147245608000  
    L_WHENCE = 1 #, % Where L_START is based.     % WHENCE              0000147345608020  
    L_START  = 2 #, % Relative offset in bytes.   % START  RETURNSTART  0000147445608040  
    L_LEN    = 3 #, % Length of area in bytes.    % LEN    RETURNLEN    0000147545608060  
    L_PID    = 4 #, % Process ID of lock holder.  % n/a.   RETURNPID    0000147645608080  
    L_PAD1   = 5 #, % For future growth.          % n/a.                0000147745608100  
    FLOCK_LEN= 6 #; % Length of "flock" structure                       0000147845608120  
                                                                        0000147945608140  
   DEFINE                                                               0000148045608160  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000148145608180  
   % Type of lock (L_TYPE) %                                            0000148245608200  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000148345608220  
      F_RDLCK   =  1 #, % reader lock                                   0000148445608240  
      F_WRLCK   =  2 #, % writer lock                                   0000148545608260  
      F_UNLCK   =  3 #; % unlock (or not locked)                        0000148645608280  
                                                                        0000148745608300  
   DEFINE                                                               0000148845608320  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000148945608340  
   %      L_WHENCE         %                                            0000149045608360  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000149145608380  
      SEEK_SET  =  0 #, % apply offset from beginning of file.          0000149245608400  
      SEEK_CUR  =  1 #, % apply offset from current file pointer pos.   0000149345608420  
      SEEK_END  =  2 #; % apply offset from EOF (byte past last written)0000149445608440  
                                                                        0000149545608460

DEFINE    ACCESS(PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE,                   0000179046100010  
                       PATH_SEARCHRULE, AMODE, ERRNO)     =             0000179146100020  
            POSIX_ACCESS(ACCESSV, PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE,  0000179246100030  
                                  PATH_SEARCHRULE, AMODE, ERRNO) #;     0000179346100040  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000179446100050  
%   ACCESS: This procedure allows the caller to determine if the        0000179546100060  
%           permissions specified via AMODE are available for the file  0000179646100070  
%           specified by PATH.                                          0000179746100080  
%                                                                       0000179846100090  
%   INPUT:  For the description of PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE, 0000179946100100  
%           and PATH_SEARCHRULE, refer to the POSIX_ACCESS Pathname     0000180046100110  
%           defines in this file.                                       0000180146100120  
%           AMODE (integer) - Specifies Bitwise inclusive OR of the     0000180246100130  
%                 following set of permissions:                         0000180346100140  
%                                                                       0000180446100150  
%               NAME/VALUE   BIT   Permission                           0000180546100160  
%               ----------  -----  ----------                           0000180646100170  
%               R_OK = 4   [2:1]  Read                                  0000180746100180  
%               W_OK = 2   [1:1]  Write                                 0000180846100190  
%               X_OK = 1   [0:1]  Execute/Search                        0000180946100200  
%               F_OK = 0    ---   Existence (always checked)            0000181046100210  
%                                                                       0000181146100220  
%   OUTPUT: none                                                        0000181246100230  
%                                                                       0000181346100240  
%   RESULT:                                                             0000181446100250  
%     If an error occurs, ERRNO is set and a -1 result is returned.     0000181546100260  
%     Otherwise 0 is returned indicating the file exists and the        0000181646100270  
%     caller has the access permissions specified by AMODE.             0000181746100280  
%     If the specified file name is shared by both a file and a         0000181846100290  
%     directory, access will be checked for the directory.              0000181946100300  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000182046100310  
                                                                        0000182146100320  
DEFINE    ALARM(SECS, ERRNO)     =                                      0000182246200000  
            POSIX_NANOALARM(SECS, ERRNO) #;                             0000182346200020  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000182446200040  
%   ALARM: causes the system to send the calling process a SIGALARM     0000182546200060  
%          signal after a specified number of seconds elapse.           0000182646200080  
%                                                                       0000182746200100  
%   INPUT: SECS (real) - number of elapsed seconds before the signal    0000182846200120  
%                        is released.                                   0000182946200140  
%                                                                       0000183046200160  
%   OUTPUT: none                                                        0000183146200180  
%                                                                       0000183246200200  
%   RETURN: Upon successful completion, the procedure returns the number0000183346200220  
%           of seconds left in the previous alarm request. The procedure0000183446200240  
%           returns 0 if there was no previous alarm request.           0000183546200260  
%           If an error occurs, the procedure returns -1 and ERRNO      0000183646200280  
%           is set to indicate the error.                               0000183746200300  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000183846200320  
                                                                        0000183946200340  
                                                                        0000184046200360  
DEFINE    CHDIR(PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN,                               0000184146202010  
                                PATH_TYPE, PATH_SEARCHRULE, ERRNO) =    0000184246202020  
POSIX_CHANGEDIR(PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN,                               0000184346202030  
                                PATH_TYPE, PATH_SEARCHRULE, ERRNO) #;   0000184446202040  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000184546202050  
%   CHDIR: changes the current working directory                        0000184646202060  
%                                                                       0000184746202070  
%   INPUT:  For the description of PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE, 0000184846202080

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000192446216200  
%   EXECVE:                                                             0000192546216210  
%     This procedure handles all the user exec interfaces.              0000192646216220  
%                                                                       0000192746216230  
%   INPUT:  For the description of PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE, 0000192846216240

%           defines in this file.                                       0000193046216260  
%     ARGV - an ebcdic array containing parameters to be passed to the  0000193146216270  
%        program being execed.                                          0000193246216280  
%     ARGV_OFF (integer) - not used (only exist for consistency).       0000193346216290  
%     ARGV_LEN (integer) - not used (only exist for consistency).       0000193446216300  
%     ARGVINDX - an integer array of indexes into ARGV which point to   0000193546216310  
%        the start of each argument.                                    0000193646216320  
%     ARGVINDX_OFF (integer) - offset into ARGVINDX of where the        0000193746216330  
%        indexes start.                                                 0000193846216340  
%     ARGVINDX_LEN (integer) - number of parameters in ARGV             0000193946216350  
%        (number of indexes).                                           0000194046216360  
%     ARGVSZ - an integer array containing to length of each parameter  0000194146216370  
%        in ARGV pointed to by the index found in ARGVINDX.             0000194246216380  
%     ARGVSZ_OFF (integer) - offset into ARGVSZ of where the sizes      0000194346216390  
%        are stored.                                                    0000194446216400  
%     ARGVSZ_LEN (integer) - number of parameters in ARGV (number       0000194546216410  
%        of sizes). Must be equal to ARGVINDX_LEN.                      0000194646216420  
%     ENVP - an ebcdic array containing environment parameters to be    0000194746216430  
%        passed to the program being execed.                            0000194846216440  
%     ENVP_OFF (integer) - not used (only exist for consistency).       0000194946216450  
%     ENVP_LEN (integer) - not used (only exist for consistency).       0000195046216460  
%     ENVPINDX - an integer array of indexes into ENVP which point to   0000195146216470

%     ENVPINDX_OFF (integer) - offset into ENVPINDX of where the indexes0000195346216490

%     ENVPINDX_LEN (integer) - number of environment parameters in ENVP 0000195546216510

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000251046450080  
%   SEMCTL: provides a set of semaphore control operations as           0000251146450100  
%           specified by the CMD parameter.                             0000251246450120  
%                                                                       0000251346450140  
%   INPUT: SEMID (integer) - semaphore id for a set                     0000251446450160

%              IPC_STAT    %read semaphore values of the semaphore set  0000251746450220  
%              IPC_SET     %set semaphore values of the semaphore set   0000251846450240  
%              IPC_RMID    %remove semaphore id from the system         0000251946450260  
%              SEM_GETNCNT %get # procs waiting for a certain sem value 0000252046450280  
%              SEM_GETPID  %get id of last process to operate on sem set0000252146450300  
%              SEM_GETVAL  %get the semaphore value                     0000252246450320  
%              SEM_GETALL  %get all semaphore values in the set         0000252346450340  
%              SEM_GETZCNT %get # procs waiting for sem value to be 0   0000252446450360  
%              SEM_SETVAL  %set a semaphore value                       0000252546450380  
%              SEM_SETALL  %set all semaphore value in the set          0000252646450400  
%          VAL (integer) - semaphore value for the SEM_SETVAL command   0000252746450420

DEFINE    SIGACTION(SIG, ACT_PROC, ACT, ACT_OFF, ACT_LEN,               0000283446660000  
                               OACT, OACT_OFF, OACT_MAX, ERRNO) =       0000283546660020

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000284046660120  
%   SIGACTION: allows the calling process to examine and/or specify     0000284146660140  
%              the action to be associated with a specific signal.      0000284246660160  
%                                                                       0000284346660180  
%   INPUT: SIG (integer) - signal number (refer to the sig values       0000284446660200  
%                          defined in this file)                        0000284546660220  
%          ACT_PROC - signal handling procedure to be invoked when a    0000284646660240  
%                     specified signal has been occurred. This procedure0000284746660260  
%                     must be declared as an integer procedure and take 0000284846660280  
%                     ten integer variables.                            0000284946660300  
%                                                                       0000285046660320  
%                INPUT:                                                 0000285146660340  
%                   INFO1 - version and signal number                   0000285246660360  
%                   INFO2 - sa_handler                                  0000285346660380  
%                   INFO3 - additional signal state information         0000285446660400  
%                   INFO4 - pid for si_pid member of siginfo structure  0000285546660420  
%                   INFO5 - uid for si_uid member of siginfo structure  0000285646660440  
%                   INFO6 - signal value for si_value member of the     0000285746660460  
%                           siginfo structure                           0000285846660480  
%                   INFO7 - for future expansion                        0000285946660500  
%                   INFO8 - for future expansion                        0000286046660520  
%                   INFO9 - for future expansion                        0000286146660540  
%                   INFO10 - for future expansion                       0000286246660560  
%                RETURN:                                                0000286346660580  
%                   result not used                                     0000286446660600  
%                                                                       0000286546660620  
%          ACT (real array) - the sigaction structure                   0000286646660640  
%              This structure contains the following 3 words:           0000286746660660  
%                sa_handler - SIG_FDFL, SIG_IGN or a unique number      0000286846660680  
%                             which is passed as the INFO2 parameter    0000286946660700  
%                             to the ACT_PROC procedure.                0000287046660720  
%                sa_mask    - a set of signals to be blocked during the 0000287146660740  
%                             execution of the signal-catching function 0000287246660760  
%                sa_flag    - flags which affect the behavior of signal 0000287346660780  
%                                                                       0000287446660800  
%          ACT_OFF (integer) - word offset into the array ACT           0000287546660820  
%                                                                       0000287646660840  
%          ACT_LEN (integer) -  the length of the sigaction structure   0000287746660860  
%              If the length is 0, then the ACT is a null structure.    0000287846660880  
%                                                                       0000287946660900  
%          OACT_OFF (integer) - word offset into the ACT structure      0000288046660920  
%          OACT_MAX (integer) - the length of the sigaction structure   0000288146660940  
%               If length is 0, then the ACT is a null structure.       0000288246660960  
%   OUTPUT:                                                             0000288346660980  
%          OACT (real array) - the sigaction structure                  0000288446661000  
%                                                                       0000288546661020  
%   RETURN: Upon successful completion, the procedure returns 0.        0000288646661040  
%           If an error occurs, the procedure returns -1 and ERRNO is   0000288746661060  
%           set to indicate the error.                                  0000288846661080

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000321046830060  
%   STAT: Returns the file status associated with the file named in     0000321146830070  
%          the PATH.                                                    0000321246830080  
%                                                                       0000321346830090  
%   INPUT:  For the description of PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE, 0000321446830100  
%           and PATH_SEARCHRULE, refer to the POSIX_ACCESS Pathname     0000321546830110  
%           defines in this file.                                       0000321646830120  
%           BUF_OFF (integer) - the word offset into the array BUF      0000321746830130  
%           BUF_MAX (integer) - max size in words starting from BUF_OFF 0000321846830140  
%                         to be filled in                               0000321946830150  
%                                                                       0000322046830160

%           If BUF_MAX does not have enough space to hold the file      0000322746830230  
%           status structure, then it will be truncated.                0000322846830240  
%           If an error occurs, the procedure returns -1 and ERRNO is   0000322946830250  
%           set to indicate the error.                                  0000323046830260  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000323146830270

INTEGER PROCEDURE STRERROR(BUFF, BUFF_OFF, BUFF_MAX, ERRNO);            0000323346850080  
VALUE                            BUFF_OFF, BUFF_MAX, ERRNO;             0000323446850100  
REFERENCE                  BUFF;                                        0000323546850120  
EBCDIC ARRAY               BUFF[0];                                     0000323646850140  
INTEGER                          BUFF_OFF, BUFF_MAX, ERRNO;             0000323746850160  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000323846850180  
%   STRERROR: converts an error number to a string and returns the      0000323946850200  
%             string in the return argument.                            0000324046850220  
%                                                                       0000324146850240  
%   INPUT: ERRNO (integer) - error number                               0000324246850260  
%                                                                       0000324346850280  
%   OUTPUT: BUFF (EBCDIC array) - place holder for an error message     0000324446850300  
%           BUFF_OFF (integer) - byte offset into the array BUFF        0000324546850320  
%           BUFF_MAX (integer) - maximum length of the array BUFF       0000324646850340  
%                                                                       0000324746850360  
%   RETURN: length of string or -1 if an error occurred.                0000324846850380  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000324946850400  
BEGIN                                                                   0000325046850420  
   EBCDIC ARRAY EA[0:POSIX_ERR_ARRAY_SIZE-1];                           0000325146850440  
   INTEGER L;                                                           0000325246850460  
                                                                        0000325346850480  
   C_STRERROR(ERRNO, EA[0], FALSE);                                     0000325446850500  
   IF ERRNO >=  0 THEN                                                  0000325546850520  
   BEGIN                                                                0000325646850540  
      SCAN EA[0] FOR L:POSIX_ERR_ARRAY_SIZE UNTIL = 0;                  0000325746850560  
      L:=POSIX_ERR_ARRAY_SIZE-L;                                        0000325846850580  
      REPLACE BUFF[BUFF_OFF] BY EA[0] FOR MIN(BUFF_MAX,L);              0000325946850600  
      STRERROR := MIN(BUFF_MAX, L);                                     0000326046850610  
   END ELSE BEGIN                                                       0000326146850620  
      STRERROR := -1;                                                   0000326246850630  
   END;                                                                 0000326346850640  
END OF STRERROR;                                                        0000326446850650  
                                                                        0000326546850660  
DEFINE SVFORK(ERRNO) =                                                  0000326646850670  
       POSIX_FORK(SVFORKV, ERRNO) #;                                    0000326746850680  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000326846850690  
%   FORK:                                                               0000326946850700  
%        This procedure creates a clone of the entire calling process   0000327046850710  
%        by executing the top frame only.  Use it if you are going to   0000327146850720  
%        call exec() right away.  The parent thread is suspended        0000327246850730  
%        until the child() does an exec() or _exit().  After an exec(), 0000327346850740  
%        but before any user code is executed, the child waits on an MCP0000327446850750  
%        event.  The parent thread must call svfork_release(<child pid>)0000327546850760  
%        to allow the child to continue.  This gives the parent time    0000327646850770  
%        to enter information about the child before the child can      0000327746850780  
%        access it or exit().                                           0000327846850790  
%                                                                       0000327946850800  
%   INPUT: none                                                         0000328046850810  
%                                                                       0000328146850820  
%   OUTPUT: none                                                        0000328246850830  
%                                                                       0000328346850840  
%   RETURN: Upon successful completion, the procedure returns 0.        0000328446850850  
%           If an error occurs, the procedure returns -1 and ERRNO is   0000328546850860  
%           set to indicate the error.                                  0000328646850870  
%           If the function succeeds, a new process will be created that0000328746850880  
%           will be returned a zero;  the calling process will be       0000328846850890  
%           returned the MIX number of the new process.                 0000328946850900  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000329046850910  
                                                                        0000329146850920  
DEFINE SVFORK_RELEASE(PID, ERRNO) =                                     0000329246850925  
       POSIX_SETIDS(RELEASEVFORK_SV, PID, 0, ERRNO) #;                  0000329346850930  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000329446850935  
%   FORK:                                                               0000329546850940  
%        Releases the child thread created by svfork().                 0000329646850945  
%                                                                       0000329746850950  
%   INPUT: PID (integer) - the child's process id that is suspended     0000329846850955  
%                          after a svfork()/exec().                     0000329946850956

           ,NIV_CD,AR_CD,NIV_CB,AR_CB,DESSC,BLOGS)=                     0000407300452102  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000407400453002  
    IF IDFAUTELIM(ALOG) > NIVLOG THEN                                   0000407500454002  
       RESULT:= 15                                                      0000407600455002  
    ELSE                                                                0000407700456002  
    IF READ(LOGO[IDFAUTELIM(ALOG)],75,BLOG) THEN                        0000407800457002  
       RESULT:= 9                                                       0000407900458002  
    ELSE                                                                0000408000459002  
    IF IDFSISTEMA(ALOG) NEQ IDFSISTEMA(BLOG) THEN                       0000408100460002  
       RESULT:= 9                                                       0000408200461002  
    ELSE                                                                0000408300462002

       RESULT:= 11                                                      0000408500464002  
    ELSE                                                                0000408600465002

       RESULT:= 12                                                      0000408800467002  
    ELSE                                                                0000408900468002

       RESULT:= 13                                                      0000409100470002  
    ELSE                                                                0000409200471002

      REPLACE DFSTATUS(BLOG) BY 02 FOR 2 DIGITS;                        0000409400473002

                                                                        0000409600475002

         NIV_CD = 0                         THEN                        0000409800479002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000409900480002

                                   FECDIA  FOR 8 DIGITS,                0000410200483002  
                                   0       FOR 8 DIGITS,                0000410300484002  
                                   0       FOR 2 DIGITS;                0000410400485002

         NIV_CD := * + 1           ;                                    0000410600487002  
         CLOSE(AR_CD,LOCK)         ;                                    0000410700487102  
      END;                                                              0000410800487202  
      IF SIST_LIB = 500 AND NIV_CB = 0 THEN                             0000410900487302  
      BEGIN                                                             0000411000487402  
        REPLACE POINTER(CD_LOG) BY 48"00" FOR 1260     ;                0000411100487502

                                   0       FOR 2 DIGITS;                0000411500488017

      END;                                                              0000411900488037  
      IF (IDFORIGEN (BLOG) = 1 OR IDFORIGEN (BLOG) = 3)  AND            0000412000488052  
          SIST_LIB = 500                                 THEN           0000412100488102  
      BEGIN                                                             0000412200488152

                                        48"00" FOR 4,                   0000412600488232  
                                        48"02" FOR 1;                   0000412700488242  
          IF NOT READ(DESSC[IDFPTRDESS(BLOG)],90,BLOGS) THEN            0000412800488252  
             REPLACE P_BLOG: P_BLOG BY POINTER(BLOGS)+4  FOR 536        0000412900488302  
          ELSE                                                          0000413000488552  
             REPLACE P_BLOG: P_BLOG BY POINTER(ALOG)+445 FOR 530;       0000413100488602  
          IF IDFEVIOCD(BLOG) = 0 AND                                    0000413200489002  
             IDFPRODUCT(BLOG)= 1 AND                                    0000413300489102  
             (IDFCVEINST(BLOG) > 10 AND IDFCVEINST(BLOG) < 16) THEN     0000413400490002  
          BEGIN                                                         0000413500491002  
             WRITE(AR_CB[NIV_CB],210,CD_LOG);                           0000413600492002  
             NIV_CB := * + 1;                                           0000413700493002

             IF IDFEVIOCD(BLOG) = 1 THEN                                0000414000495002  
             BEGIN                                                      0000414100496002  
                WRITE(AR_CB[NIV_CB],210,CD_LOG);                        0000414200497002  
                NIV_CB := * + 1;                                        0000414300498002

                BEGIN                                                   0000414600498302  
                   WRITE(AR_CD[NIV_CD],210,CD_LOG);                     0000414700498402  
                   NIV_CD := * + 1;                                     0000414800498502  
                END;                                                    0000414900498602  
%FIN ISOLATION BNE 2023 R10                                             0000415000498702  
             END                                                        0000415100499002  
             ELSE                                                       0000415200499052  
                IF IDFEVIOCD(BLOG) = 2 THEN                             0000415300499102  
                BEGIN                                                   0000415400499152  
                   WRITE(AR_CD[NIV_CD],210,CD_LOG);                     0000415500499202  
                   WRITE(AR_CB[NIV_CB],210,CD_LOG);                     0000415600499252  
                   NIV_CD := * + 1;                                     0000415700499302  
                   NIV_CB := * + 1;                                     0000415800499352  
                END                                                     0000415900499402  
                ELSE                                                    0000416000499452  
                   IF IDFEVIOCD(BLOG) = 3 THEN                          0000416100499502  
                   BEGIN                                                0000416200499552  
                      WRITE(AR_CD[NIV_CD],210,CD_LOG);                  0000416300499602  
                      NIV_CD := * + 1;                                  0000416400499652  
                   END                                                  0000416500499702  
%INI ISOLATION BNE 2023 R10                                             0000416600499712  
                   ELSE                                                 0000416700499722  
                      IF IDFEVIOBN(BLOG) = 1 THEN                       0000416800499742  
                      BEGIN                                             0000416900499762  
                         WRITE(AR_CD[NIV_CD],210,CD_LOG);               0000417000499782  
                         NIV_CD := * + 1;                               0000417100499802  
                      END;                                              0000417200499822  
%FIN ISOLATION BNE 2023 R10                                             0000417300499922  
         END;                                                           0000417400500002  
%                                                                       0000417500501002  
      WHILE IDFAUTANT(BLOG) NEQ 0 DO                                    0000417600502002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000417700503002  
         AUT_ANTER:=IDFAUTANT(BLOG);                                    0000417800504002

             IF IDFEVIOCD(BLOG) = 0 AND                                 0000419400508302

                (IDFCVEINST(BLOG) > 10 AND IDFCVEINST(BLOG) < 16) THEN  0000419600508402  
             BEGIN                                                      0000419700508502

  BORRAXPROC(LOGO,ALOG,FECDIA,CD_LOG,NIV_CD,AR_CD,NIV_CB,AR_CB)=        0000427000546002  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000427100547002  
     NUM_REG:=0;                                                        0000427200548002  
     REG_LEIDOS:=-1;                                                    0000427300549002  
     CLOSE (LOGO,LOCK);                                                 0000427400550002  
     LOGO.BLOCKSIZE := 150 * BLOQUEO_BATCH;                             0000427500551002  
     IF SIST_LIB = 500 THEN                                             0000427600552002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000427700553002  
        CLOSE(AR_CD,LOCK);                                              0000427800554002  
        AR_CD.BLOCKSIZE := 10500;                                       0000427900555002  
     END;                                                               0000428000556002  
     IF SIST_LIB = 500 THEN                                             0000428100556102  
     BEGIN                                                              0000428200556202  
        CLOSE(AR_CB,LOCK);                                              0000428300557002  
        AR_CB.BLOCKSIZE := 10500;                                       0000428400557102  
     END;                                                               0000428500557202  
    IF FEC_X_PROC[NUMDIAC1,PROCAELIM] = 0 THEN                          0000428600557212  
    ELSE                                                                0000428700557222  
    BEGIN                                                               0000428800557232  
     REG_LEIDOS := FEC_X_PROC[NUMDIAC1,PROCAELIM] - 1;                  0000428900557242  
     WHILE NOT READ(LOGO[REG_LEIDOS:= * + 1],75,ALOG) AND               0000429000558002  
            INTEGER(POINTER(ALOG,4),2) NEQ 99 DO                        0000429100559002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000429200560002  
       IF     IDFFUNCION(ALOG)  = 1                AND                  0000429300561002  
%             IDFSISTEMA(ALOG)  = IDFSISTEMA(ALOG) AND                  0000429400562002  
              IDFTIPPROC(ALOG)  = PROCAELIM        AND                  0000429500563002  
              IDFSTATUS (ALOG)  = 1               THEN                  0000429600564002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000429700565002  
            NUM_REG:= * + 1;                                            0000429800566002  
            REPLACE POINTER(ALOG,4)+ 899 BY 3 FOR 1 DIGITS;             0000429900567002  
            WRITE(LOGO[REG_LEIDOS],75,ALOG);                            0000430000568002  
            IF SIST_LIB = 500  AND                                      0000430100569002  
               (IDFORIGEN (ALOG)  = 1     OR                            0000430200570002  
                IDFORIGEN (ALOG)  = 3 )   THEN                          0000430300571002  
            BEGIN                                                       0000430400572002  
               REPLACE POINTER(CD_LOG) BY 48"00" FOR 1260;              0000430500573002  
               REPLACE POINTER(ALOG,4)+ 899 BY 2 FOR 1 DIGITS;          0000430600574002  
               REPLACE POINTER(CD_LOG) BY POINTER(ALOG) FOR 1260;       0000430700575002  
               CASE IDFEVIOCD(ALOG) OF                                  0000430800576002  
               BEGIN                                                    0000430900576102  
                  2 : BEGIN                                             0000431000576202  
                         WRITE(AR_CD[NIV_CD],210,CD_LOG);               0000431100576302  
                         WRITE(AR_CB[NIV_CB],210,CD_LOG);               0000431200577002  
                         NIV_CD := * + 1;                               0000431300578002  
                         NIV_CB := * + 1;                               0000431400579002  
                      END;                                              0000431500580002  
                  3 : BEGIN                                             0000431600581002  
                         WRITE(AR_CD[NIV_CD],210,CD_LOG);               0000431700581052  
                         NIV_CD := * + 1;                               0000431800581102  
                      END;                                              0000431900581152  
                  1 : BEGIN                                             0000432000581202  
                         WRITE(AR_CB[NIV_CB],210,CD_LOG);               0000432100581252  
                         NIV_CB := * + 1;                               0000432200581302  
%INI ISOLATION BNE 2023 R10                                             0000432300581307  
                         IF IDFEVIOBN(ALOG) = 1 THEN                    0000432400581312  
                         BEGIN                                          0000432500581317  
                            WRITE(AR_CD[NIV_CD],210,CD_LOG);            0000432600581322  
                            NIV_CD := * + 1;                            0000432700581327  
                         END;                                           0000432800581332  
%FIN ISOLATION BNE 2023 R10                                             0000432900581337  
                      END;                                              0000433000581352  
                  ELSE:                                                 0000433100581402  
                  BEGIN                                                 0000433200581412  
                     IF IDFPRODUCT(ALOG)  = 1  AND                      0000433300581422  
                        IDFCVEINST(ALOG) > 10  AND                      0000433400581452  
                        IDFCVEINST(ALOG) < 16  THEN                     0000433500581502  
                        BEGIN                                           0000433600581552  
                           WRITE(AR_CB[NIV_CB],210,CD_LOG);             0000433700581602  
                           NIV_CB := * + 1;                             0000433800581652  
                        END;                                            0000433900581702  
%INI ISOLATION BNE 2023 R10                                             0000434000581703  
                     IF IDFEVIOBN(ALOG) = 1 THEN                        0000434100581704  
                     BEGIN                                              0000434200581705  
                        WRITE(AR_CD[NIV_CD],210,CD_LOG);                0000434300581706  
                        NIV_CD := * + 1;                                0000434400581707  
                     END;                                               0000434500581708  
%FIN ISOLATION BNE 2023 R10                                             0000434600581709  
                  END;                                                  0000434700581712  
               END;                                                     0000434800581802  
            END;                                                        0000434900582002  
       END;                                                             0000435000583002  
     END;                                                               0000435100584002  
    END;                                                                0000435200584012  
     CLOSE (LOGO,LOCK);                                                 0000435300585002  
     LOGO.BLOCKSIZE := 150 * BLOQUEO_LINEA;                             0000435400586002  
     IF SIST_LIB = 500 THEN                                             0000435500587002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000435600588002  
        CLOSE(AR_CD,LOCK);                                              0000435700589002  
       AR_CD.BLOCKSIZE := 210 * (BLOQUEO_LINEA + 1);                    0000435800590002  
     END;                                                               0000435900591002

  BORRAXAUT(LOGO,ALOG,FECDIA,CD_LOG,NIV_CD,AR_CD,NIV_CB,AR_CB)=         0000437500602002  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000437600603002  
     NUM_REG:=0;                                                        0000437700604002  
     REG_LEIDOS:=AUTAELIM;                                              0000437800605002  
     CLOSE (LOGO,LOCK);                                                 0000437900606002

     END;                                                               0000438500612002  
     IF SIST_LIB = 500 THEN                                             0000438600612102  
     BEGIN                                                              0000438700612202  
        CLOSE(AR_CB,LOCK);                                              0000438800612302  
        AR_CB.BLOCKSIZE := 10500;                                       0000438900612402  
     END;                                                               0000439000612502  
     WHILE NOT READ(LOGO[REG_LEIDOS:= * + 1],75,ALOG) AND               0000439100613002  
            INTEGER(POINTER(ALOG,4),2) NEQ 99 DO                        0000439200614002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000439300615002  
       IF     IDFFUNCION(ALOG)  = 1                AND                  0000439400616002  
              IDFTIPPROC(ALOG)  = PROCAELIM        AND                  0000439500617002

       BEGIN                                                            0000439700619002  
            NUM_REG:= * + 1;                                            0000439800620002  
            REPLACE POINTER(ALOG,4)+ 899 BY 4 FOR 1 DIGITS;             0000439900621002

            IF SIST_LIB = 500    AND                                    0000440100623002  
              (IDFORIGEN (ALOG)  = 1     OR                             0000440200624002  
               IDFORIGEN (ALOG)  = 3 )   THEN                           0000440300625002

               BEGIN                                                    0000440900630022  
                  2 : BEGIN                                             0000441000630042  
                         WRITE(AR_CD[NIV_CD],210,CD_LOG);               0000441100630062  
                         WRITE(AR_CB[NIV_CB],210,CD_LOG);               0000441200630102  
                         NIV_CD := * + 1;                               0000441300631002  
                         NIV_CB := * + 1;                               0000441400631102

                         NIV_CD := * + 1;                               0000441800632102  
                      END;                                              0000441900632202  
                  1 : BEGIN                                             0000442000632302  
                         WRITE(AR_CB[NIV_CB],210,CD_LOG);               0000442100633002  
                         NIV_CB := * + 1;                               0000442200633102  
%INI ISOLATION BNE 2023 R10                                             0000442300633112  
                         IF IDFEVIOBN(ALOG) = 1 THEN                    0000442400633122  
                         BEGIN                                          0000442500633132  
                            WRITE(AR_CD[NIV_CD],210,CD_LOG);            0000442600633142  
                            NIV_CD := * + 1;                            0000442700633152  
                         END;                                           0000442800633162  
%FIN ISOLATION BNE 2023 R10                                             0000442900633172  
                      END;                                              0000443000633202  
                  ELSE:                                                 0000443100633302  
                  BEGIN                                                 0000443200633312  
                     IF IDFPRODUCT(ALOG)  = 1  AND                      0000443300633402  
                        IDFCVEINST(ALOG) > 10  AND                      0000443400634002  
                        IDFCVEINST(ALOG) < 16  THEN                     0000443500634102  
                        BEGIN                                           0000443600634202

                        WRITE(AR_CD[NIV_CD],210,CD_LOG);                0000444300636042  
                        NIV_CD := * + 1;                                0000444400636052  
                     END;                                               0000444500636062  
%FIN ISOLATION BNE 2023 R10                                             0000444600636072  
                  END;                                                  0000444700636102  
               END;                                                     0000444800637002  
            END;                                                        0000444900637102  
       END;                                                             0000445000637202  
     END;                                                               0000445100638002

     BEGIN                                                              0000445500642002

     BEGIN                                                              0000446000645202  
        CLOSE(AR_CB,LOCK);                                              0000446100645302

  DFAMPLIAFILE(FILE_AMP,LEV_INI,ADD_REC,ARR_VACIO, REG_SIZE)=           0000448200664002  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000448300665002  
     ERRORMSG;                                                          0000448400666002  
%    REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ARCHIVO "             ,                         0000448500667002  
%                      FILE_AMP.TITLE         ,                         0000448600668002  
%                      " EXCEDIO SU CAPACIDAD, INICIA AMPLIACION ";     0000448700669002  
%    LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                   0000448800670002  
     REPLACE POINTER(ARR_VACIO,4) BY 99 FOR 2 DIGITS;                   0000448900671002  
     CLOSE(FILE_AMP,LOCK) ;                                             0000449000672002  
     BLOCK_SIZE := FILE_AMP.BLOCKSIZE;                                  0000449100673002  
%    IF BLOCK_SIZE = (150 * 4) OR                                       0000449200673102  
%       BLOCK_SIZE = 330 THEN                                           0000449300673152

     ACQUIREL(E,MIX_LOCK)=                                              0000508701144002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000508801145002  
      TOTAL := 0;                                                       0000508901146002  
      RES_LOCK := LOCK(E,TIEMPO);                                       0000509001147002  
      WHILE RES_LOCK > 1 DO                                             0000509101148002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000509201149002  
         TOTAL := * + TIEMPO;                                           0000509301150002  
         IF TOTAL > 15 THEN                                             0000509401151002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000509501152002  
            ERRORMSG;                                                   0000509601153002  
            REPLACE TXTMSG BY "Llevo "                    ,             0000509701154002  
                              TOTAL FOR 4 DIGITS          ,             0000509801155002  
                              " Seg en espera del Lock,"  ,             0000509901156002  
                              " La mezcla "               ,             0000510001157002  
                              MIX_LOCK_ANT FOR 4 DIGITS   ,             0000510101158002  
                              " Lo tiene tomado"          ;             0000510201159002  
            LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                            0000510301160002  
        END;                                                            0000510401161002  
        RES_LOCK := LOCK(E,TIEMPO);                                     0000510501162002  
      END;                                                              0000510601163002  
      MIX_LOCK_ANT := MIX_LOCK;                                         0000510701163012  
     END#,                                                              0000510801164002

     DFVALIDA(ALOG,SISTEMA,PRODUCTO,GIND)=                              0000511901175002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000512001176002  
       RESULT:=0;                                                       0000512101177002  
                                                                        0000512201177302  
       IF IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG,1) = 0 THEN                                   0000512301178002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000512401179002  
          ERRORMSG;                                                     0000512501180002  
%         REPLACE TXTMSG BY "CVE ERRONEA = ", NUM_CLAVE FOR 4 DIGITS,   0000512601181002  
%                           ", INDICE  1" ;                             0000512701182002  
%         LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                              0000512801183002  
          RESULT := 3;                                                  0000512901184002  
       END;                                                             0000513001185002  
       IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000513101186002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000513201187002  
          GIND:= 0;                                                     0000513301188002  
%         IND_CAT :=0;                                                  0000513401189002  
          WHILE GIND:= * + 1          LEQ 5 AND                         0000513501190002  
                IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG,GIND)   > 0 DO                          0000513601191002  
            BEGIN                                                       0000513701192002  
                NUM_CLAVE := IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG,GIND);                     0000513801193002  
                IF IADFCVECOL(NUM_CLAVE) LEQ 0 THEN                     0000513901194002  
                BEGIN                                                   0000514001195002  
                   ERRORMSG;                                            0000514101196002  
%                  REPLACE TXTMSG BY "CVE ERRONEA = ",                  0000514201197002  
%                                    NUM_CLAVE FOR 4 DIGITS ,           0000514301198002  
%                                    ", INDICE ",GIND FOR 2 DIGITS;     0000514401199002  
%                  LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                     0000514501200002  
                   RESULT := 3;                                         0000514601201002  
                END;                                                    0000514701202002  
            END;                                                        0000514801203002  
       END;                                                             0000514901204002  
       IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000515001205002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000515101206002  
          GIND:= 0;                                                     0000515201207002  
          WHILE GIND:= * + 1          LEQ 5 AND                         0000515301208002  
                IDFIMPOCUR(ALOG,GIND)   > 0 DO                          0000515401209002  
            BEGIN                                                       0000515501210002  
                NUM_CLAVE := IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG,GIND);                     0000515601211002  
                                                                        0000515701212002  
                IF (IADFCVEINDIC(NUM_CLAVE) = 2  OR                     0000515801213002  
                    IADFCVEINDIC(NUM_CLAVE) = 3) AND                    0000515901214002  
                    IDFSUCPROM(ALOG)            LEQ  0  THEN            0000516001215002  
                    RESULT := 5;                                        0000516101216002  
                IF (IADFCVEINDIC(NUM_CLAVE) = 1  OR                     0000516201217002  
                    IADFCVEINDIC(NUM_CLAVE) = 3) AND                    0000516301218002  
                    IDFSUCINI(ALOG)              LEQ 0  THEN            0000516401219002  
                    RESULT := 6;                                        0000516501220002  
                                                                        0000516601221002  
            END;                                                        0000516701222002  
       END;                                                             0000516801223002  
       IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000516901224002  
       IF IDFORIGEN(ALOG) = 1 OR                                        0000517001225002  
          IDFORIGEN(ALOG) = 2 OR                                        0000517101226002  
          IDFORIGEN(ALOG) = 3 THEN                                      0000517201227002  
       ELSE                                                             0000517301228002  
          RESULT:= 10;                                                  0000517401229002  
                                                                        0000517501230002  
       IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000517601231002  
          IF IDFMONEDA(ALOG) > 0 THEN                                   0000517701232002  
          ELSE RESULT := 14;                                            0000517801233002  
                                                                        0000517901234002  
     END#,                                                              0000518001235002

%***********************************                                    0000558901562002  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE CALINDSIS(SISTEMA,PRODUCTO);                         0000559001563002  
 VALUE   SISTEMA,PRODUCTO;                                              0000559101564002  
 INTEGER SISTEMA,PRODUCTO;                                              0000559201565002  
%***********************************                                    0000559301566002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000559401567002  
   CASE SISTEMA OF                                                      0000559501568002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000559601569002  
     500:CASE PRODUCTO OF                                               0000559701570002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000559801571002  
            066:CALINDSIS:=0;                                           0000559901572002  
            500:CALINDSIS:=1;                                           0000560001573002  
            001:CALINDSIS:=2;                                           0000560101574002  
           ELSE:CALINDSIS:=99;                                          0000560201575002  
         END;                                                           0000560301576002  
     084:CASE PRODUCTO OF                                               0000560401577002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000560501578002

%*******************************************************************    0000563601609002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000563701610002  
    STAT_P015[IND_COPIA].STATUS := VALUE(NEVERUSED);                    0000563801611002  
    REPLACE NOM_ASINC BY "(S151)S151/OBJECT/P015/ASINCS",               0000563901612002  
                           SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,                       0000564001613002  
                   FEC_DIA[IND_COPIA] FOR 6 DIGITS,                     0000564101614002  
                           "/",                                         0000564201615002  
                           MTP_P015 FOR 8,                              0000564301616002  
                           " ON CMEMP.";                                0000564401617002  
                                                                        0000564501618002  
    REPLACE STAT_P015[IND_COPIA].NAME BY NOM_ASINC;                     0000564601619002  
    REPLACE PARAM BY 48"00" FOR 200;                                    0000564701619102  
    REPLACE PARAM BY FEC_DIA[IND_COPIA] FOR 8 DIGITS ,                  0000564801620002  
                IND_COPIA+1             FOR 2 DIGITS ,                  0000564901621002  
%               SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,");";                             0000565001622002  
                SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS;                                  0000565101622052  
    PROCESS EJECUTA(PARAM)[STAT_P015[IND_COPIA]];                       0000565201623002  
    INTENTOS := 0;                                                      0000565301623102  
    WHILE STATUS_MIX_P015(IND_COPIA) < 1 AND                            0000565401623202  
          INTENTOS < 30                  DO                             0000565501623302  
    BEGIN                                                               0000565601623402  
       WAIT((.5))        ;                                              0000565701624002  
       INTENTOS := * + 1 ;                                              0000565801624102  
       IF INTENTOS > 28 THEN                                            0000565901624112  
       BEGIN                                                            0000566001624122  
          REPLACE MSGLJ BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ);                         0000566101624132

                  "SE LEVANTE EL P015";                                 0000566301624152  
          LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                              0000566401624162  
       END;                                                             0000566501624182  
    END;                                                                0000566601624202  
    IF STATUS_MIX_P015(IND_COPIA) > 0 THEN                              0000566701624302  
    BEGIN                                                               0000566801624402  
       REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                               0000566901625002  
       REPLACE ARR1 BY "REMOVE ",                                       0000567001626002  
                       "(S151)S151/OBJECT/P015/ASINCS",                 0000567101627002  
                         SIST_LIB          FOR 3 DIGITS,                0000567201628002

                         MTP_P015 FOR 8,                                0000567501631002  
                         " ON CMEMP"                                    0000567601632002

    END;                                                                0000567901634102  
 END OF EXTERNO_P015;                                                   0000568001635002

 BOOLEAN PROCEDURE EXTERNO_P025;                                        0000571801638002  
%*******************************************************************    0000571901639002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000572001640002  
    STAT_P025.STATUS := VALUE(NEVERUSED);                               0000572101641002  
    REPLACE CTLVER_ID BY "S151P025ACTS";                                0000572201642002  
    IF DAME_TIT(CTLVER_ID,CTLVER_NOM) < 0  THEN                         0000572301643002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000572401644002  
       REPLACE MSGLJ BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ);                            0000572501645002  
       REPLACE MSGLJ BY HDRMSG,"I>020901< LIBRARY REGISTRA, ",          0000572601646002  
               "ERROR EN CTLVERS, NO EXISTE IDENTIFICADOR DE ",         0000572701647002  
               "P025 ASINCRONO S151 (S151P025ACT)" ;                    0000572801648002  
       LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                 0000572901649002  
     REPLACE NOMP025 BY "(S151)S151/OBJECT/P025/ACTBDSDOMOV/01MTP   ";  0000573001650002  
    END                                                                 0000573101651002  
    ELSE                                                                0000573201652002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000573301653002  
       REPLACE NOMP025 BY CTLVER_NOM UNTIL = " ", " ";                  0000573401654002  
       POSICION := SIZE(NOMP025);                                       0000573501655002  
       IF POSICION > 16 THEN                                            0000573601656002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000573701657002  
          POINT_P025 := NOMP025[POSICION - 16];                         0000573801658002  
          REPLACE MTP_P025 BY POINT_P025 FOR 8;                         0000573901659002  
       END                                                              0000574001660002  
       ELSE                                                             0000574101661002  
          REPLACE MTP_P025 BY "01MTP   ";                               0000574201662002  
    END;                                                                0000574301663002  
    REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                                  0000574401664002  
    REPLACE ARR1 BY "COPY ", NOMP025 UNTIL = " " ,                      0000574501665002  
                    " AS ",                                             0000574601666002  
                    "(S151)S151/OBJECT/P025/ACTBDS",                    0000574701667002  
                      SIST_LIB          FOR 3 DIGITS,                   0000574801668002  
                      "/",                                              0000574901669002  
                      MTP_P025 FOR 8,                                   0000575001670002  
                      " FROM CMEMP(PACK) TO CMEMP(PACK)",               0000575101671002  
                      48"00";                                           0000575201672002  
    DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                        0000575301673002  
    REPLACE NOM_ASINC BY "(S151)S151/OBJECT/P025/ACTBDS",               0000575401674002  
                          SIST_LIB          FOR 3 DIGITS,               0000575501675002  
                          "/",                                          0000575601676002  
                          MTP_P025 FOR 8,                               0000575701677002  
                          " ON CMEMP.",                                 0000575801678002  
                          48"00";                                       0000575901679002  
    REPLACE STAT_P025.NAME BY NOM_ASINC;                                0000576001680002  
    REPLACE PARAM BY SIST_LIB          FOR 3 DIGITS ;                   0000576101681002  
    PROCESS EJECUTA(PARAM)[STAT_P025];                                  0000576201682002  
    WAIT((3));                                                          0000576301683002  
 END OF EXTERNO_P025;                                                   0000576401684002
