%----------------------                                                 0000651201707002  
 PROCEDURE CARGAMEMORY;                                                 0000651301708002  
%----------------------                                                 0000651401709002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000651501710002  
   IF IDFFUNCION(ALOG1) = 1 THEN                                        0000651601711002  
      DFVALIDA(ALOG1,IDFSISTEMA(ALOG1),IDFPRODUCT(ALOG1),GIND1);        0000651701712002  
      IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                                0000651801713002  
         GRABALOG(LOGS  [NUMDIAC1],ALOG1,NIVLOG_A[NUMDIAC1],            0000651901714002  
                  FEC_DIA [NUMDIAC1],BLOG1,DESS [NUMDIAC1],             0000652001715002  
                  NIVDES_A [NUMDIAC1],LOG_CD,NIVLOG_CD[NUMDIAC1],       0000652101715102  
                  CDIR[NUMDIAC1],NIVLOG_CB[NUMDIAC1],CBII[NUMDIAC1]);   0000652201716002  
%NOR-ISI INICIO SE ALMACENAN NO. DE AUT PARA OPTIMIZACION DE FUNCION 21 0000652301716012  
      IF IDFFUNCION(ALOG1) = 1 THEN                                     0000652401716014  
      BEGIN                                                             0000652501716016  
         IF FEC_X_PROC[NUMDIAC1,IDFTIPPROC(BLOG1)] = 0 THEN             0000652601716022  
         BEGIN                                                          0000652701716032  
            FEC_X_PROC[NUMDIAC1,IDFTIPPROC(BLOG1)] := IDFAUT(BLOG1);    0000652801716042  
            IND_PROC := -1;                                             0000652901716052  
            P_FECHAS := POINTER(ARR_PROCESOS);                          0000653001716062  
            WHILE IND_PROC < 100 DO                                     0000653101716072  
            BEGIN                                                       0000653201716082  
               REPLACE P_FECHAS:P_FECHAS  BY                            0000653301716092  
                       FEC_X_PROC[NUMDIAC1,IND_PROC] FOR 8 DIGITS;      0000653401716102  
               IND_PROC := * + 1;                                       0000653501716112  
            END;                                                        0000653601716122  
            WRITE(PROCESOS[NUMDIAC1],150,ARR_PROCESOS[0]);              0000653701716132  
            REPLACE ARR_PROCESOS[0] BY 48"00" FOR 150 WORDS;            0000653801716142  
         END;                                                           0000653901716152  
      END;                                                              0000654001716162  
%NOR-ISI FIN                                                            0000654101716172  
                                                                        0000654201717102  
   IF LAST_RLOG[NUMDIAC1] - NIVLOG_A[NUMDIAC1] < 300 THEN               0000654301718002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000654401719002  
      REGSIZE := 75;                                                    0000654501719102  
      DFAMPLIAFILE(LOGS[NUMDIAC1],LAST_RLOG[NUMDIAC1],AMPL_REG,A_VACIO, 0000654601720002  
                   REGSIZE);                                            0000654701720102  
       READ(LOGS[NUMDIAC1][0]);                                         0000654801721002  
      LAST_RLOG[NUMDIAC1]:=LOGS[NUMDIAC1].LASTRECORD;                   0000654901722002  
   END;                                                                 0000655001723002  
                                                                        0000655101724002  
                                                                        0000655201725002  
   IF LAST_RDES[NUMDIAC1] - NIVDES_A[NUMDIAC1] < 300 THEN               0000655301726002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000655401727002  
      REGSIZE:=90;                                                      0000655501727102  
      DFAMPLIAFILE(DESS[NUMDIAC1],LAST_RDES[NUMDIAC1],AMPL_REG,A_VACIO, 0000655601728002  
                   REGSIZE);                                            0000655701728102  
      READ(DESS[NUMDIAC1][0]);                                          0000655801729002  
      LAST_RDES[NUMDIAC1]:=DESS[NUMDIAC1].LASTRECORD;                   0000655901730002  
   END;                                                                 0000656001731002  
   IF SIST_LIB          = 500 THEN                                      0000656101731102  
      IF CBII[NUMDIAC1].RESIDENT THEN                                   0000656201731302  
         IF LAST_RCB2[NUMDIAC1] - NIVLOG_CB[NUMDIAC1] < 300 THEN        0000656301732022  
         BEGIN                                                          0000656401732042  
            REGSIZE:= 210;                                              0000656501732062  
            AMPL_REG_CB2CTD := 2000;                                    0000656601732082  
            DFAMPLIAFILE(CBII[NUMDIAC1],LAST_RCB2[NUMDIAC1],            0000656701732102  
                         AMPL_REG_CB2CTD, B_VACIO, REGSIZE);            0000656801732122  
            READ(CBII[NUMDIAC1][0]);                                    0000656901732142  
            LAST_RCB2[NUMDIAC1]:=CBII[NUMDIAC1].LASTRECORD;             0000657001732162  
         END;                                                           0000657101732182  
   IF SIST_LIB          = 500 THEN                                      0000657201732206  
      IF CDIR[NUMDIAC1].RESIDENT THEN                                   0000657301732207  
         IF LAST_RCTD[NUMDIAC1] - NIVLOG_CD[NUMDIAC1] < 300 THEN        0000657401732242  
         BEGIN                                                          0000657501732262  
            REGSIZE:= 210;                                              0000657601732282  
            AMPL_REG_CB2CTD := 2000;                                    0000657701732302

            READ(CDIR[NUMDIAC1][0]);                                    0000658001732362  
            LAST_RCTD[NUMDIAC1]:=CDIR[NUMDIAC1].LASTRECORD;             0000658101732382  
         END;                                                           0000658201732402  
 END OF CARGAMEMORY;                                                    0000658301733002

 PROCEDURE REBLOCKADE;                                                  0000667101810002  
%---------------------                                                  0000667201811002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000667301812002  
   INTEGER BLOQUE;                                                      0000667401813002  
   CAUSE(RESET_EVE);                                                    0000667501814002  
   CARGAMEMORY ;                                                        0000667601815002  
   IF IDFFUNCION(ALOG1)        = 31 THEN                                0000667701816002  
      IF LOGS[NUMDIAC1].BLOCKSIZE = (150 * BLOQUEO_BATCH) THEN          0000667801816102  
                                                                        0000667901816202  
      ELSE                                                              0000668001816302  
      BEGIN                                                             0000668101817002  
         CLOSE (LOGS[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                   0000668201818002  
         CLOSE (DESS[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                   0000668301819002  
         LOGS[NUMDIAC1].BLOCKSIZE := 150 * BLOQUEO_BATCH;               0000668401819102  
         DESS[NUMDIAC1].BLOCKSIZE := 90  * 120;                         0000668501819202  
         IF SIST_LIB = 500 THEN                                         0000668601820202  
         BEGIN                                                          0000668701820252  
            CLOSE (CBII[NUMDIAC1],LOCK)      ;                          0000668801820302  
            CLOSE(CDIR[NUMDIAC1],LOCK)       ;                          0000668901823042  
            CBII[NUMDIAC1].BLOCKSIZE := 10500;                          0000669001823092  
            CDIR[NUMDIAC1].BLOCKSIZE := 10500;                          0000669101823102  
         END;                                                           0000669201823172  
         IF LOGS[NUMDIAC1].RESIDENT THEN                                0000669301824002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000669401825002  
            READ(LOGS[NUMDIAC1][0]);                                    0000669501826002  
            LAST_RLOG[NUMDIAC1]:=LOGS[NUMDIAC1].LASTRECORD;             0000669601827002  
         END;                                                           0000669701828002  
         IF DESS[NUMDIAC1].RESIDENT THEN                                0000669801829002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000669901830002  
            READ(DESS[NUMDIAC1][0]);                                    0000670001831002  
            LAST_RDES[NUMDIAC1]:=DESS[NUMDIAC1].LASTRECORD;             0000670101832002  
         END;                                                           0000670201833002  
      END;                                                              0000670301834002  
                                                                        0000670401835002  
   IF IDFFUNCION(ALOG1)        = 32 THEN                                0000670501836002  
      IF LOGS[NUMDIAC1].BLOCKSIZE = (150 * BLOQUEO_LINEA) THEN          0000670601836102  
                                                                        0000670701836202  
      ELSE                                                              0000670801836302  
      BEGIN                                                             0000670901837002  
         CLOSE (LOGS[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                   0000671001838002  
         CLOSE (DESS[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                   0000671101839002  
         LOGS[NUMDIAC1].BLOCKSIZE := 150 * BLOQUEO_LINEA;               0000671201839102  
         DESS[NUMDIAC1].BLOCKSIZE := 90  * BLOQUEO_LINEA * 2;           0000671301839202  
         IF SIST_LIB = 500 THEN                                         0000671401840002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000671501840102  
           CLOSE (CDIR[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                 0000671601840202  
           CLOSE (CBII[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                 0000671701840302  
           CBII[NUMDIAC1].BLOCKSIZE :=210 * (BLOQUEO_LINEA + 1);        0000671801840402  
           CDIR[NUMDIAC1].BLOCKSIZE :=210 * (BLOQUEO_LINEA + 1);        0000671901840502  
         END;                                                           0000672001840602  
         IF LOGS[NUMDIAC1].RESIDENT THEN                                0000672101844002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000672201845002  
            READ(LOGS[NUMDIAC1][0]);                                    0000672301846002  
            LAST_RLOG[NUMDIAC1]:=LOGS[NUMDIAC1].LASTRECORD;             0000672401847002  
         END;                                                           0000672501848002  
         IF DESS[NUMDIAC1].RESIDENT THEN                                0000672601849002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000672701850002  
            READ(DESS[NUMDIAC1][0]);                                    0000672801851002  
            LAST_RDES[NUMDIAC1]:=DESS[NUMDIAC1].LASTRECORD;             0000672901852002  
         END;                                                           0000673001853002  
      END;                                                              0000673101854002  
   RESET(RESET_EVE);                                                    0000673201855002  
 END OF REBLOCKADE;                                                     0000673301856002

%---------------------                                                  0000676301882002  
                                                                        0000676401883002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000676501884002

   CARGAMEMORY ;                                                        0000676701886002  
   REINTENTOS := 0;                                                     0000676801892002  
   RESULT_CTL := 1;                                                     0000676901893002  
   WHILE RESULT_CTL > 0 AND REINTENTOS < 3 DO                           0000677001894002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000677101895002  
      REPLACE ACONTROLE1 BY 0 FOR SIZE(ACONTROLE1);                     0000677201896002  
      REPLACE FUNB01F05(ACONTROLE1) BY    05 FOR 2 DIGITS,              0000677301897002  
                                    SIST_LIB FOR 4 DIGITS,              0000677401898002  
                                    CSI_LIB  FOR 2 DIGITS,              0000677501899002  
                                          00 FOR 2 DIGITS;              0000677601900002  
      RESULT_CTL := CONSISDIA(ACONTROLE1);                              0000677701901002  
      REINTENTOS := * + 1;                                              0000677801902002  
   END;                                                                 0000677901903002  
   IF RESULT_CTL > 0 THEN                                               0000678001904002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000678101905002  
     REPLACE MSGLJ BY HDRMSG,"I>020901<";                               0000678201906002  
     REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR EN FUNCION 98 ",                          0000678301907002  
                       "L002/REGISTRAS",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,          0000678401908002  
                       " CSI ", CSI_LIB          FOR 2 DIGITS,          0000678501909002  
                      ", RESULT L001/CONTROL ", RESULT_CTL FOR 6 DIGITS,0000678601910002  
                      " NO SE MODIF. ESTATUS DE PROY. FECHA ",          0000678701911002  
                      " PARA ", ISISB01F05(ACONTROLE1) FOR 3 DIGITS;    0000678801912002  
     LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                   0000678901913002  
   END;                                                                 0000679001914002  
                                                                        0000679101915002  
 END OF FINAL;                                                          0000679201916002

         END;                                                           0000699101976100

            IF SIST_LIB = 701 THEN                                      0000700901977102  
            BEGIN                                                       0000701001977202  
               NUM_SIS := 701;                                          0000701101977302  
               REPLACE CTLVER_ID BY "S151LOTE    ";                     0000701201977402

                                     "START ",                          0000702801978902

                  ZIP WITH ARR_ZIP;                                     0000703601979037

             END;                                                       0000704901979090  
         END                                                            0000705001979202  
    ELSE:RESULT:= 2;                                                    0000705101999002  
    END;                                                                0000705202000002  
    REPLACE HHMMSS BY TIME(9) FOR 6   ;                                 0000705302000102  
    HORA := INTEGER(POINTER(HHMMSS),6);                                 0000705402000202  
    IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                                  0000705502001002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000705602002002  
       IF FUNCIONVAL < 98                   AND                         0000705702002012  
          (STATUS_MIX_P015(NUMDIAC1) < 1 OR                             0000705802002014

          LEVANTA_P015                      THEN                        0000706302005002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000706402006002

             VERSION_P016;                                              0000707402044402  
             IND_COPIA := NUMDIAC1;                                     0000707502044602  
             ACTIVA_P016 := TRUE;                                       0000707602044652  
          END;                                                          0000707702044702  
          IF ACTIVA_P015 OR ACTIVA_P016 THEN CAUSE(LEVANTA_PASOS);      0000707802044802  
                                                                        0000707902044902  
       END;                                                             0000708002045002  
       IF STATUS_MIX_P025 < 1 AND FEC_DIA[NUMDIAC1] = FECS151           0000708102046002  
          AND  FUNCIONVAL = 1 AND IDFTIPPROC(ALOG1) > 19 AND            0000708202047002  
          STATUS_BDSDO < 99 THEN                                        0000708302048002  
          EXTERNO_P025;                                                 0000708402049002  
                                                                        0000708502050002  
%%%%%  CAUSE (HAYMOV[NUMDIAC1]);                                        0000708602051002  
    END;                                                                0000708702052002  
 END;                                                                   0000708802053002  
 IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                                     0000708902054002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000709002055002  
    ACT_NIVEL := * + 1;                                                 0000709102056002  
    IF IDFFUNCION(ALOG1) = 1 OR IDFFUNCION(ALOG1) = 2                   0000709202057002

          REG_X_BLOCK[NUMDIAC1] := * + 1;                               0000711202071202

          BEGIN                                                         0000711402071242  
             REG_X_BLOCK_D         := 0;                                0000711502071262  
             REG_X_BLOCK[NUMDIAC1] := 0;                                0000711602071282  
             CAUSE(BLOCK_LLENO);                                        0000711702071302  
          END;                                                          0000711802071322  
       END;                                                             0000711902071342  
    END                                                                 0000712002071362  
    ELSE                                                                0000712102071382  
    BEGIN                                                               0000712202071402  
       IF IDFTIPPROC(ALOG1) < 20 THEN                                   0000712302071422  
       BEGIN                                                            0000712402071442  
          REG_X_BLOCK_DX        := * + 1;                               0000712502071462  
          REG_X_BLOCK[NUMDIAC1] := * + 1;                               0000712602071482  
          IF REG_X_BLOCK[NUMDIAC1] = (BLOQUEO_LINEA * 2) THEN           0000712702071502  
             REG_X_BLOCK[NUMDIAC1] := 0;                                0000712802071522  
       END;                                                             0000712902071542  
    END;                                                                0000713002071562  
 END;                                                                   0000713102072002  
 IF RESULT > 0 THEN                                                     0000713202073002

%INICIO CJMG CAMBIO PARA PROYECTO TESOFE 2019                           0000720602136502  
% IF SIZESDOS1 > 216 THEN                                               0000720702137002  
%    SIZESDOS1:= 216;                                                   0000720802138002

    SIZESDOS1:= 390;                                                    0000721102139202

%INICIO CJMG CAMBIO PARA PROYECTO TESOFE 2019                           0000721302139502

 BEGIN                                                                  0000721802144002

    BEGIN                                                               0000722002146002  
       NUM_FEC  := J_J ;                                                0000722102147002  
       BOOL_J   := TRUE;                                                0000722202148002  
    END;                                                                0000722302149002  
 END;                                                                   0000722402150002  
 IF NOT BOOL_J THEN                                                     0000722502151002  
    RESULT_SDO := 4                                                     0000722602152002  
 ELSE                                                                   0000722702153002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000722802154002  
    CASE IDFSDOFUNC(ASDO1) OF                                           0000722902155002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000723002156002  
       80:BEGIN                                                         0000723102157002  
             NIVSDO_A[NUM_FEC] := 0   ;                                 0000723202158002  
             CLOSE (SDOS[NUM_FEC]);                                     0000723302159002  
             SDOS[NUM_FEC].MYUSE:= 1   ;                                0000723402160002  
%INICIO CJMG CAMBIO PROYECTO TESOFE 2019                                0000723502160502

%FIN CJMG CAMBIO PROYECTO TESOFE 2019                                   0000723702161502  
             CARGAMEMSDO;                                               0000723802162002  
             CLOSE (SDOS[NUM_FEC],LOCK);                                0000723902163002  
             SDOS[NUM_FEC].MYUSE:= 3   ;                                0000724002164002  
          END;                                                          0000724102165002  
       81:CARGAMEMSDO;                                                  0000724202166002  
       82:BEGIN                                                         0000724302167002  
             CARGAMEMSDO;                                               0000724402168002  
             CLOSE (SDOS[NUM_FEC],LOCK);                                0000724502169002  
             IF SIST_LIB = 500 THEN                                     0000724602169102  
                FUNCION_82 := TRUE;                                     0000724702169202  
          END;                                                          0000724802170002  
       83:BEGIN                                                         0000724902170102  
             CARGAMEMSDO;                                               0000725002170202  
             CLOSE (SDOS[NUM_FEC],LOCK);                                0000725102170302  
             IF SIST_LIB = 500 THEN                                     0000725202170322  
             BEGIN                                                      0000725302170332  
                FUNCION_83 := TRUE;                                     0000725402170342  
                REPLACE ARCH_FN83 BY "(S151)S151/FILE/S500/FUN83/",     0000725502170347  
                                      FEC_DIA[NUM_FEC] FOR 8 DIGITS,    0000725602170352  
                                     " ON CMEMP."                 ;     0000725702170357  
                REPLACE FINSDO.TITLE BY ARCH_FN83;                      0000725802170362  
                OPEN(FINSDO,WAIT); CLOSE(FINSDO,LOCK);                  0000725902170367  
             END;                                                       0000726002170372  
          END;                                                          0000726102170402  
       ELSE:RESULT_SDO:= 2;                                             0000726202171002  
    END;                                                                0000726302172002  
 END;                                                                   0000726402173002  
 IF RESULT_SDO > 0 THEN                                                 0000726502174002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000726602175002  
       REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1,4)+1078 BY RESULT_SDO FOR 2 DIGITS;        0000726702183002  
       WRITE(ERRORES1[NIVERROR1],90,ASDO1);                             0000726802184002  
       NIVERROR1 := * + 1;                                              0000726902185002  
    END;                                                                0000727002186002

  CASE FUNCION OF                                                       0000732902214002  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000733002215002  
     1:                                                                 0000733102216002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000733202217002

         BEGIN                                                          0000734102226002

                              ", NIVEL = "                    ,         0000734402229002

                              NIVLOG_CB[K]  FOR 6 DIGITS, " " ,         0000734602229022  
                              NIVLOG_CD[K]  FOR 6 DIGITS      ;         0000734702229032  
            LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                            0000734802230002  
         END;                                                           0000734902231002  
         REPLACE MSGLJ BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ)  ;                        0000735002231050  
         REPLACE MSGLJ BY HDRMSG, "I>050110<";                          0000735102231100  
         REPLACE TXTMSG BY "** NIVELES DE ACTUALIZACION EN FILE **",    0000735202231150  
                           "** DESCRIPCIONES    : ";                    0000735302231200  
         LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                               0000735402231250  
         K:=-1;                                                         0000735502231300  
         WHILE K:= * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                       0000735602231350  
         BEGIN                                                          0000735702231400  
            REPLACE TXTMSG BY "** FECHA ", K+1  FOR * DIGITS, " ",      0000735802231450  
                                  FEC_DIA[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS,             0000735902231500  
                    ", NIVEL = ", NIVDES_A[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS, " **";     0000736002231550  
            LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                            0000736102231600  
         END;                                                           0000736202231650  
         RESULT:=0                                                      0000736302232002  
       END;                                                             0000736402233002  
                                                                        0000736502234002  
     2:                                                                 0000736602235002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000736702236002  
         REPLACE MSGLJ BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ)  ;                        0000736802237002  
          REPLACE MSGLJ BY HDRMSG, "I>050110<";                         0000736902238002  
         REPLACE TXTMSG BY "** NIVELES DE ACTUALIZACION EN FILE **",    0000737002239002

%REPLACE PTR_HEX:PTR_HEX     BY 000000000 FOR 8 DIGITS,                 0000806102635002  
%                               000000000 FOR 8 DIGITS,                 0000806202636002

                                00      FOR 2 DIGITS;                   0000806402638002  
 SIST := 151;                                                           0000806502639002  
 RESULTADO := 0;                                                        0000806602640002  
 PROG := 009;                                                           0000806702641002

 BEGIN                                                                  0000807102645002  
    RESULTADO := L710_CONSUL_DETALLE(SIST,PROG,PAR_ENT,PAR_SAL);        0000807202646002

    PTR_EBC := PAR_ENT[6];                                              0000807702651002

    FECINI:= IDFSALFECONS(REG);                                         0000807902652002

    PTR_EBC := PAR_ENT[6];                                              0000808202665002  
    REPLACE PTR_EBC:PTR_EBC BY DFSAL_DATOS(PAR_SAL,NREG_CAT,1) FOR 12,  0000808302666002  
                               DFSAL_DATOS(PAR_SAL,NREG_CAT,2) FOR 12,  0000808402667002  
                               DFSAL_DATOS(PAR_SAL,NREG_CAT,3) FOR 12,  0000808502668002  
                               DFSAL_DATOS(PAR_SAL,NREG_CAT,4) FOR 12,  0000808602669002  
                               DFSAL_DATOS(PAR_SAL,NREG_CAT,5) FOR 12,  0000808702670002  
                               DFSAL_DATOS(PAR_SAL,NREG_CAT,6) FOR 12;  0000808802671002  
    IF RESULTADO > 1 AND NOT BOOL_CAT THEN                              0000808902672002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000809002673002  
       REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR LIB/REGISTRAS",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,   0000809102674002  
                         ", CATALOGO S080 VACIO, ",                     0000809202675002  
                         " NO EXISTEN CLAVES DE TRANSACCION PARA S",    0000809302676002  
                         SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS;                         0000809402677002  
       LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                 0000809502678002  
    END;                                                                0000809602679002  
    IF RESULTADO = 0 THEN RESULTADO:=3;                                 0000809702680002  
    BOOL_CAT:=TRUE;                                                     0000809802681002  
    IND_RENGLON := 1;                                                   0000809902681102  
    WHILE IND_RENGLON < (NREG_CAT + 1) DO                               0000810002681202  
    BEGIN                                                               0000810102681302  
       NAT     := IDFSAL_DATOS(PAR_SAL,IND_RENGLON,11);                 0000810202681402  
       INDS250 := IDFSAL_DATOS(PAR_SAL,IND_RENGLON,12);                 0000810302681502  
                                                                        0000810402681602  
       IF FORMATO = 2 THEN                                              0000810502681702  
          CLAVETR:=IDFSAL_DATOS(PAR_SAL,IND_RENGLON,2)                  0000810602681802  
       ELSE                                                             0000810702681902  
          CLAVETR:=IDFSAL_DATOS(PAR_SAL,IND_RENGLON,1);                 0000810802682002  
%DISPLAY(STRING(CLAVETR,4)!!" "!!STRING(NAT,2)!!" "!!STRING(INDS250,2));0000810902682102  
       IF NAT = 1 OR NAT = 2 THEN                                       0000811002682202  
          IF INDS250 = 2 THEN                                           0000811102683002  
             REPLACE ADFCVEINDIC(CLAVETR) BY 3   FOR 1 DIGITS           0000811202684002  
          ELSE                                                          0000811302685002  
             REPLACE ADFCVEINDIC(CLAVETR) BY 1   FOR 1 DIGITS           0000811402686002  
       ELSE                                                             0000811502687002  
          IF INDS250 = 2 THEN                                           0000811602688002  
             REPLACE ADFCVEINDIC(CLAVETR) BY 2   FOR 1 DIGITS;          0000811702689002  
                                                                        0000811802690002  
       REPLACE ADFCVECOL  (CLAVETR) BY NAT FOR 1 DIGITS;                0000811902691002  
       IND_RENGLON := * + 1 ;                                           0000812002691102  
    END;                                                                0000812102691202  
 END;                                                                   0000812202692002  
 END OF CARGACVES;                                                      0000812302693002  
                                                                        0000812402694002

 PROCEDURE LEVANTA;                                                     0000812602696002  
%******************                                                     0000812702697002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000812802698002  
   DEFINE HFUNCION    = INTEGER(POINTER(ALOG,4),2)#,                    0000812902699002  
                                                                        0000813002700002  
          HFECLOG     = INTEGER((POINTER(ALOG)+2),8)#,                  0000813102701002  
          HNIVLOG     = INTEGER((POINTER(ALOG)+10),8)#;                 0000813202702002  
   ARRAY   ALOG[0:59];                                                  0000813302703002  
   INTEGER I,J,RESULT,RESULT_CTL,K,INT;                                 0000813402704002  
   INTEGER ARRAY NIVEL_K[0:9];                                          0000813502705002  
   INTEGER NIV_INF, NIV_SUP,NUM_REG;                                    0000813602706002

%----------------------------------------------                         0000813802708002  
 PROCEDURE ARMALOG(LOGN,NIVLOG,FECDIA,NIVBASE);                         0000813902709002  
%----------------------------------------------                         0000814002710002  
 VALUE LOGN,FECDIA,NIVBASE;                                             0000814102711002  
 INTEGER LOGN,NIVLOG,FECDIA,NIVBASE;                                    0000814202712002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000814302713002  
   LABEL VERIFICA;                                                      0000814402714002  
                                                                        0000814502715002  
   INTEGER I;                                                           0000814602716002  
                                                                        0000814702717002  
   NIVLOG:= 0;                                                          0000814802718002  
   VERIFICA:                                                            0000814902719002  
                                                                        0000815002720002  
                                                                        0000815102721002  
   REPLACE NOM_ARCMOV BY ARC_MOV FOR 34,                                0000815202722002  
                         FECDIA  FOR 6 DIGITS,                          0000815302723002  
                         " ON "              ,                          0000815402724002  
                         PK_MOV  FOR 17,"."  ;                          0000815502725002  
%FLH                    "CMEMP"  FOR  5,"."  ;                          0000815602725102  
   IF LOGS[LOGN].OPEN THEN                                              0000815702726002  
      IF LOGS[LOGN].RESIDENT THEN                                       0000815802726012  
         CLOSE(LOGS[LOGN])                                              0000815902726022  
      ELSE                                                              0000816002726032  
      BEGIN                                                             0000816102726042  
         CLOSE(LOGS[LOGN],LOCK);                                        0000816202726052  
         OPEN (LOGS[LOGN],WAIT);                                        0000816302726062  
         CLOSE(LOGS[LOGN])     ;                                        0000816402726072  
      END;                                                              0000816502726082  
                                                                        0000816602726102  
   REPLACE LOGS[LOGN].TITLE BY NOM_ARCMOV;                              0000816702727002  
   LOGS[LOGN].MYUSE:= 3;                                                0000816802728002  
   IF LOGS[LOGN].RESIDENT THEN                                          0000816902731002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000817002732002  
     READ(LOGS[LOGN][0],75,ALOG);                                       0000817102733002  
     LAST_RLOG[LOGN]:=LOGS[LOGN].LASTRECORD;                            0000817202734002  
     IF HFUNCION NEQ 99 THEN                                            0000817302735002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000817402736002  
        WHILE HFECLOG NEQ FECDIA DO                                     0000817502737002  
        BEGIN                                                           0000817602738002  
          CLOSE(LOGS[LOGN],LOCK);                                       0000817702739002  
       REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR1 LIB/REGISTRAS",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,  0000817802740002  
                            ", FECHA HEADER FILE/MOVTOS: ",             0000817902741002  
                            POINTER(ALOG)+2 FOR 8,                      0000818002742002  
                            " FECHA CORRECTA: ",                        0000818102743002  
                            FECDIA FOR 8 DIGITS;                        0000818202744002  
          LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                              0000818302745002  
          ACCEPT(TXTMSG);                                               0000818402746002  
          LOGS[LOGN].MYUSE:= 3;                                         0000818502747002  
          READ(LOGS[LOGN][0],75,ALOG);                                  0000818602748002  
        END;                                                            0000818702749002  
        NIV_SUP:=LOGS[LOGN].LASTRECORD;                                 0000818802750002  
     END                                                                0000818902751002  
     ELSE                                                               0000819002752002  
        NIV_SUP:=0;                                                     0000819102753002  
     NIV_INF:=0;                                                        0000819202755002  
                                                                        0000819302756002  
     WHILE NIV_INF NEQ NIV_SUP DO                                       0000819402757002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000819502758002  
          NUM_REG:=INTEGERT((NIV_SUP - NIV_INF)/2) + NIV_INF;           0000819602759002  
           READ(LOGS[LOGN][NUM_REG],75,ALOG);                           0000819702760002  
                                                                        0000819802761002  
          IF HFUNCION = 99   THEN                                       0000819902762002  
             NIV_SUP := NUM_REG                                         0000820002763002  
          ELSE                                                          0000820102764002  
             NIV_INF := NUM_REG;                                        0000820202765002  
                                                                        0000820302766002  
          IF NIV_SUP - NIV_INF = 1 THEN                                 0000820402767002  
             NIV_SUP := NIV_INF;                                        0000820502768002  
                                                                        0000820602769002  
       END;                                                             0000820702770002  
     NIVLOG:=NIV_SUP;                                                   0000820802771002  
     J := NIVLOG;                                                       0000820902772002  
      READ(LOGS[LOGN][J:=*+1],75,ALOG);                                 0000821002773002  
     WHILE HFUNCION NEQ 99 DO                                           0000821102774002  
        IF READ(LOGS[LOGN][J:=*+1],75,ALOG) THEN                        0000821202775002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000821302776002  
         REGSIZE := 75;                                                 0000821402780102  
         DFAMPLIAFILE(LOGS[LOGN],J,AMPL_REG,A_VACIO,REGSIZE);           0000821502781002  
         READ(LOGS[LOGN][0]);                                           0000821602782002  
         LAST_RLOG[LOGN]:=LOGS[LOGN].LASTRECORD;                        0000821702783002  
       END;                                                             0000821802797002  
   END                                                                  0000821902798002  
   ELSE                                                                 0000822002799002  
   IF ( NIVBASE > 0              AND                                    0000822102800002  
       (FECDIA  = FEC_PRO_BASE  OR                                      0000822202800102  
        FECDIA  > FEC_PRO_BASE)) AND                                    0000822302800202  
       (IDFFUNCION(ALOG) < 95 OR                                        0000822402800302  
        IDFFUNCION(ALOG) > 95)   THEN                                   0000822502800402  
                                                                        0000822602800502  
   BEGIN                                                                0000822702801002  
                                                                        0000822802802002  
      REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR : FALTA ARCHIVO ",                       0000822902803002  
                        LOGS[LOGN].TITLE        ,                       0000823002804002  
                        ", FAVOR DE COPIARLO   ";                       0000823102805002  
                                                                        0000823202806002  
      LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                  0000823302807002  
      ACCEPT(TXTMSG);                                                   0000823402808002  
      GO VERIFICA;                                                      0000823502809002  
   END;                                                                 0000823602810002  
 END OF ARMALOG;                                                        0000823702811002  
                                                                        0000823802812002  
%----------------------------------------------                         0000823902813002  
 PROCEDURE ARMADES(LOGN,NIVDES,FECDIA,NIVSDO);                          0000824002814002  
%----------------------------------------------                         0000824102815002  
 VALUE   LOGN,FECDIA;                                                   0000824202816002  
 INTEGER LOGN,NIVDES,FECDIA,NIVSDO;                                     0000824302817002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000824402818002  
%  DEFINE IDFFECDES = INTEGER((POINTER(ALOG)+2) ,6)#,                   0000824502819002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000824602820002  
   DEFINE IDFFECDES = INTEGER((POINTER(ALOG)+2) ,8)#,                   0000824702821002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000824802822002

           DFFECDES =          POINTER(ALOG)+2     #,                   0000825002824002

          IDFSTADES = INTEGER((POINTER(ALOG)+18),2)#;                   0000825302827002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000825402828002  
   LABEL VERIFICA;                                                      0000825502829002  
   INTEGER I;                                                           0000825602830002  
                                                                        0000825702831002  
   NIVDES :=0;                                                          0000825802832002  
   VERIFICA:                                                            0000825902833002  
   REPLACE NOM_ARCDES BY ARC_DES FOR 34,                                0000826002834002  
                         FECDIA  FOR 6 DIGITS,                          0000826102835002  
                         " ON "              ,                          0000826202836002  
                         PK_DES  FOR 17,"."  ;                          0000826302837002  
%FLH                    "CMEMP"  FOR 5 ,"."  ;                          0000826402837102  
   IF DESS[LOGN].OPEN THEN                                              0000826502838002  
      IF DESS[LOGN].RESIDENT THEN                                       0000826602838012  
         CLOSE(DESS[LOGN])                                              0000826702838022  
      ELSE                                                              0000826802838032  
      BEGIN                                                             0000826902838037  
         CLOSE(DESS[LOGN],LOCK);                                        0000827002838042  
         OPEN (DESS[LOGN],WAIT);                                        0000827102838052  
         CLOSE(DESS[LOGN])     ;                                        0000827202838062  
      END;                                                              0000827302838072  
                                                                        0000827402838102  
   REPLACE DESS[LOGN].TITLE BY NOM_ARCDES;                              0000827502839002  
                                                                        0000827602840002  
   REPLACE NOM_ARCSDO BY ARC_SDO FOR 34,                                0000827702841002  
                         FECDIA  FOR 6 DIGITS,                          0000827802842002  
                         " ON "              ,                          0000827902843002  
                         PK_SDO  FOR 17,"."  ;                          0000828002844002  
   IF SDOS[LOGN].OPEN THEN                                              0000828102845002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000828202845102  
      IF SDOS[LOGN].RESIDENT THEN                                       0000828302845202  
         CLOSE(SDOS[LOGN])                                              0000828402845302  
      ELSE                                                              0000828502845402  
         CLOSE(SDOS[LOGN],LOCK) ;                                       0000828602845502  
      SDOS[LOGN].MYUSE := 3;                                            0000828702845602  
   END;                                                                 0000828802845702  
   REPLACE SDOS[LOGN].TITLE BY NOM_ARCSDO;                              0000828902846002  
                                                                        0000829002849002  
%  IF SDOS[LOGN].RESIDENT THEN                                          0000829102850002  
%  BEGIN                                                                0000829202851002  
%     SDOS[LOGN].MYUSE := 3;                                            0000829302852002  
%     READ(SDOS[LOGN][0],31,ASDO);                                      0000829402853002  
%     WHILE IDFFECSDO NEQ FECDIA DO                                     0000829502854002  
%     BEGIN                                                             0000829602855002  
%        CLOSE(SDOS[LOGN],LOCK);                                        0000829702856002  
%        ERRORMSG;                                                      0000829802857002  
%        REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR LIB/REGISTRAS",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS, 0000829902858002  
%                                                                       0000830002859002  
%                          ", FECHA HEADER FILE/SDO : ",                0000830102860002  
%                          DFFECSDO   FOR 8,                            0000830202861002  
%                        " FECHA CORRECTA : ",                          0000830302862002  
%                        FECDIA     FOR 8 DIGITS;                       0000830402863002  
%      LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                 0000830502864002  
%      ACCEPT(TXTMSG);                                                  0000830602865002  
%      SDOS[LOGN].MYUSE:= 3;                                            0000830702866002  
%      READ(SDOS[LOGN][0],31,ASDO);                                     0000830802867002  
%    END;                                                               0000830902868002  
%    NIVSDO:=SDOS[LOGN].LASTRECORD;                                     0000831002869002  
%  END                                                                  0000831102870002  
%  ELSE                                                                 0000831202871002  
%  BEGIN                                                                0000831302872002  
%    SDOS[LOGN].MYUSE := 1 ;                                            0000831402873002  
%    NIVSDO:= 0;                                                        0000831502874002  
%  END;                                                                 0000831602875002  
   NUM_REG:=0;                                                          0000831702876002  
   IF DESS[LOGN].RESIDENT THEN                                          0000831802879002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000831902880002  
     READ(DESS[LOGN][0],90,ALOG);                                       0000832002881002  
     LAST_RDES[LOGN]:=DESS[LOGN].LASTRECORD;                            0000832102882002  
     IF HFUNCION  NEQ 99 THEN                                           0000832202883002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000832302884002  
       WHILE HFECLOG NEQ FECDIA DO                                      0000832402885002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000832502886002  
         CLOSE(DESS[LOGN],LOCK);                                        0000832602887002  
         REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR LIB/REGISTRAS",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS, 0000832702888002  
                         ", FECHA HEADER FILE/DES : ",                  0000832802889002  
                         DFFECDES   FOR 8,                              0000832902890002  
                         " FECHA CORRECTA : ",                          0000833002891002  
                         FECDIA     FOR 8 DIGITS;                       0000833102892002  
         LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                               0000833202893002  
         ACCEPT(TXTMSG);                                                0000833302894002  
         DESS[LOGN].MYUSE:= 3;                                          0000833402895002  
         READ(DESS[LOGN][0],90,ALOG);                                   0000833502896002  
       END;                                                             0000833602897002  
       NIV_SUP:=DESS[LOGN].LASTRECORD;                                  0000833702898002  
     END                                                                0000833802899002  
     ELSE                                                               0000833902900002  
        NIV_SUP:=0;                                                     0000834002901002  
                                                                        0000834102902002  
     NIV_INF:=0;                                                        0000834202903002  
                                                                        0000834302904002  
     WHILE NIV_INF NEQ NIV_SUP DO                                       0000834402905002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000834502906002  
          NUM_REG:=INTEGERT((NIV_SUP - NIV_INF)/2) + NIV_INF;           0000834602907002  
            READ(DESS[LOGN][NUM_REG],90,ALOG);                          0000834702908002  
                                                                        0000834802909002  
          IF HFUNCION = 99   THEN                                       0000834902910002  
             NIV_SUP := NUM_REG                                         0000835002911002  
          ELSE                                                          0000835102912002  
             NIV_INF := NUM_REG;                                        0000835202913002  
                                                                        0000835302914002  
          IF NIV_SUP - NIV_INF = 1 THEN                                 0000835402915002  
             NIV_SUP := NIV_INF;                                        0000835502916002  
                                                                        0000835602917002  
       END;                                                             0000835702918002  
     NIVDES:=NIV_SUP;                                                   0000835802919002  
     J := NIVDES;                                                       0000835902920002  
       READ(DESS[LOGN][J:=*+1],90,ALOG);                                0000836002921002  
     WHILE HFUNCION NEQ 99 DO                                           0000836102922002  
         IF READ(DESS[LOGN][J:=*+1],90,ALOG) THEN                       0000836202923002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000836302924002  
         REGSIZE :=90;                                                  0000836402928102  
         DFAMPLIAFILE(DESS[LOGN],J,AMPL_REG,A_VACIO,REGSIZE);           0000836502929002  
         READ(DESS[LOGN][0]);                                           0000836602930002  
         LAST_RDES[LOGN]:=DESS[LOGN].LASTRECORD;                        0000836702931002  
       END;                                                             0000836802945002  
   END                                                                  0000836902946002  
   ELSE                                                                 0000837002947002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000837102948002  
     IF LOGS[LOGN].RESIDENT THEN                                        0000837202949002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000837302950002  
        READ(LOGS[LOGN][0],75,ALOG);                                    0000837402951002  
       IF IDFSTADES = 1 THEN                                            0000837502952002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000837602953002  
         ERRORMSG;                                                      0000837702954002  
         REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR LIB/REGISTRAS",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS, 0000837802955002  
                           ", FALTA ", DESS[LOGN].TITLE ,               0000837902956002  
                           ", FAVOR DE COPIARLO "      ,                0000838002957002  
                           FECDIA FOR 8 DIGITS;                         0000838102958002  
         LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                               0000838202959002  
         ACCEPT(TXTMSG) ;                                               0000838302960002  
         GO VERIFICA;                                                   0000838402961002  
       END                                                              0000838502962002  
       ELSE                                                             0000838602963002  
          NIVDES:= 0;                                                   0000838702964002  
     END                                                                0000838802965002  
     ELSE                                                               0000838902966002  
        NIVDES:= 0;                                                     0000839002967002  
   END;                                                                 0000839102968002  
 END OF ARMADES;                                                        0000839202969002

%----------------------------------------------                         0000839402969082  
 PROCEDURE ARMA_CBII(NUM_CBLOG,NIV_CB,FECDIA,NIVBASE);                  0000839502969112  
%----------------------------------------------                         0000839602969142  
 VALUE NUM_CBLOG,FECDIA,NIVBASE;                                        0000839702969172  
 REFERENCE NIV_CB;                                                      0000839802969202  
 INTEGER NUM_CBLOG,NIV_CB,FECDIA,NIVBASE;                               0000839902969232  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000840002969262  
   LABEL VERIFICA_CB;                                                   0000840102969292  
                                                                        0000840202969322  
   INTEGER I;                                                           0000840302969352  
   NIV_CB:= 0;                                                          0000840402969382  
   VERIFICA_CB:                                                         0000840502969412  
   REPLACE NOM_ARCMOV BY "(S151)S151/FILE/CB2S",                        0000840602969442  
                         SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,                         0000840702969472  
                         "/",                                           0000840802969502  
                         FECDIA  FOR 6 DIGITS,                          0000840902969532  
                         " ON "              ,                          0000841002969562  
                         PK_MOV  FOR 17,"."  ;                          0000841102969592  
%FLH                     "CMEMP"  FOR  5,"."  ;                         0000841202969612  
   IF CBII[NUM_CBLOG].OPEN THEN                                         0000841302969622  
   BEGIN                                                                0000841402969627  
      CLOSE(CBII[NUM_CBLOG]);                                           0000841502969632  
      CBII[NUM_CBLOG].MYUSE:= 3;                                        0000841602969637  
   END;                                                                 0000841702969642  
   REPLACE CBII[NUM_CBLOG].TITLE BY NOM_ARCMOV;                         0000841802969652  
                                                                        0000841902969682  
   IF CBII[NUM_CBLOG].RESIDENT THEN                                     0000842002969772  
   BEGIN                                                                0000842102969802  
     READ(CBII[NUM_CBLOG][0],210,ALOG);                                 0000842202969832  
     LAST_RCB2[NUM_CBLOG] := CBII[NUM_CBLOG].LASTRECORD;                0000842302969852  
     IF HFUNCION NEQ 99 THEN                                            0000842402969862  
     BEGIN                                                              0000842502969892  
        WHILE HFECLOG NEQ FECDIA DO                                     0000842602969922  
        BEGIN                                                           0000842702969952  
          CLOSE(CBII[NUM_CBLOG],LOCK);                                  0000842802969982  
        REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR2 LIB/REGISTRAS",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS, 0000842902970012  
                            ", FECHA HEADER FILE/CB2: ",                0000843002970042  
                            POINTER(ALOG)+2 FOR 8,                      0000843102970072  
                            " FECHA CORRECTA: ",                        0000843202970102  
                            FECDIA FOR 8 DIGITS;                        0000843302970132  
          LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                              0000843402970162  
          CLOSE(CBII[NUM_CBLOG],LOCK);                                  0000843502970192  
          ACCEPT(TXTMSG);                                               0000843602970222  
          CBII[NUM_CBLOG].MYUSE:= 3;                                    0000843702970252  
          READ(CBII[NUM_CBLOG][0],210,ALOG);                            0000843802970282  
        END;                                                            0000843902970312  
        NIV_SUP:=CBII[NUM_CBLOG].LASTRECORD;                            0000844002970342  
     END                                                                0000844102970372  
     ELSE                                                               0000844202970402  
        NIV_SUP:=0;                                                     0000844302970432  
                                                                        0000844402970462  
     NIV_INF:=0;                                                        0000844502970492  
     IF NIV_INF NEQ NIV_SUP THEN                                        0000844602970522  
     BEGIN                                                              0000844702970552  
        WHILE NIV_INF NEQ NIV_SUP DO                                    0000844802970582  
        BEGIN                                                           0000844902970612  
          NUM_REG:=INTEGERT((NIV_SUP - NIV_INF)/2) + NIV_INF;           0000845002970642  
          READ(CBII[NUM_CBLOG][NUM_REG],210,ALOG);                      0000845102970672  
                                                                        0000845202970702  
          IF HFUNCION = 99   THEN                                       0000845302970732  
             NIV_SUP := NUM_REG                                         0000845402970762  
          ELSE                                                          0000845502970792  
             NIV_INF := NUM_REG;                                        0000845602970822  
                                                                        0000845702970852  
          IF NIV_SUP - NIV_INF = 1 THEN                                 0000845802970882  
             NIV_SUP := NIV_INF;                                        0000845902970912  
                                                                        0000846002970942  
       END;                                                             0000846102970972  
       NIV_CB:=NIV_SUP + 1;                                             0000846202971002  
     END                                                                0000846302971032  
     ELSE                                                               0000846402971062  
        NIV_CB := NIV_SUP;                                              0000846502971092  
   END                                                                  0000846602971122  
   ELSE                                                                 0000846702971152  
   IF NIVBASE > 0 AND                                                   0000846802971182  
     (IDFFUNCION(ALOG) < 95 OR                                          0000846902971192  
      IDFFUNCION(ALOG) > 95)    THEN                                    0000847002971202

                        CBII[NUM_CBLOG].TITLE        ,                  0000847302971272

%----------------------------------------------                         0000848102972002  
 PROCEDURE ARMA_CDIR(NUM_CDLOG,NIV_CD,FECDIA,NIVBASE);                  0000848202973002  
%----------------------------------------------                         0000848302974002  
 VALUE NUM_CDLOG,FECDIA,NIVBASE;                                        0000848402975002  
 REFERENCE NIV_CD;                                                      0000848502976002  
 INTEGER NUM_CDLOG,NIV_CD,FECDIA,NIVBASE;                               0000848602977002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000848702978002  
   LABEL VERIFICA_CD;                                                   0000848802979002  
                                                                        0000848902980002  
   INTEGER I;                                                           0000849002981002  
   NIV_CD:= 0;                                                          0000849102982002  
   VERIFICA_CD:                                                         0000849202983002  
   REPLACE NOM_ARCMOV BY "(S151)S151/FILE/CTDS",                        0000849302984002  
                         SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,                         0000849402985002  
                         "/",                                           0000849502986002  
                         FECDIA  FOR 6 DIGITS,                          0000849602987002  
                         " ON "              ,                          0000849702988002  
                         PK_MOV  FOR 17,"."  ;                          0000849802989002  
%FLH                     "CMEMP" FOR 5 ,"."  ;                          0000849902989102  
                                                                        0000850002990002  
   IF CDIR[NUM_CDLOG].OPEN THEN                                         0000850102990102  
   BEGIN                                                                0000850202990202  
      CLOSE(CDIR[NUM_CDLOG])   ;                                        0000850302990302  
      CDIR[NUM_CDLOG].MYUSE:= 3;                                        0000850402990402  
   END;                                                                 0000850502990502  
   REPLACE CDIR[NUM_CDLOG].TITLE BY NOM_ARCMOV;                         0000850602991002  
                                                                        0000850702994002  
   IF CDIR[NUM_CDLOG].RESIDENT THEN                                     0000850802995002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000850902996002  
     READ(CDIR[NUM_CDLOG][0],210,ALOG);                                 0000851002997002  
     LAST_RCTD[NUM_CDLOG] := CDIR[NUM_CDLOG].LASTRECORD;                0000851102997102  
     IF HFUNCION NEQ 99 THEN                                            0000851202998002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000851302999002  
        WHILE HFECLOG NEQ FECDIA DO                                     0000851403000002  
        BEGIN                                                           0000851503001002  
          CLOSE(CDIR[NUM_CDLOG],LOCK);                                  0000851603002002  
        REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR2 LIB/REGISTRAS",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS, 0000851703003002  
                            ", FECHA HEADER FILE/CDIR: ",               0000851803004002  
                            POINTER(ALOG)+2 FOR 8,                      0000851903005002  
                            " FECHA CORRECTA: ",                        0000852003006002  
                            FECDIA FOR 8 DIGITS;                        0000852103007002  
          LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                              0000852203008002  
          CLOSE(CDIR[NUM_CDLOG],LOCK);                                  0000852303009002  
          ACCEPT(TXTMSG);                                               0000852403010002  
          CDIR[NUM_CDLOG].MYUSE:= 3;                                    0000852503011002  
          READ(CDIR[NUM_CDLOG][0],210,ALOG);                            0000852603012002  
        END;                                                            0000852703013002  
        NIV_SUP:=CDIR[NUM_CDLOG].LASTRECORD;                            0000852803014002  
     END                                                                0000852903015002  
     ELSE                                                               0000853003016002  
        NIV_SUP:=0;                                                     0000853103017002  
                                                                        0000853203018002  
     NIV_INF:=0;                                                        0000853303019002  
     IF NIV_INF NEQ NIV_SUP THEN                                        0000853403020002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000853503021002  
        WHILE NIV_INF NEQ NIV_SUP DO                                    0000853603022002  
        BEGIN                                                           0000853703023002  
          NUM_REG:=INTEGERT((NIV_SUP - NIV_INF)/2) + NIV_INF;           0000853803024002  
          READ(CDIR[NUM_CDLOG][NUM_REG],210,ALOG);                      0000853903025002  
                                                                        0000854003026002  
          IF HFUNCION = 99   THEN                                       0000854103027002  
             NIV_SUP := NUM_REG                                         0000854203028002  
          ELSE                                                          0000854303029002  
             NIV_INF := NUM_REG;                                        0000854403030002  
                                                                        0000854503031002  
          IF NIV_SUP - NIV_INF = 1 THEN                                 0000854603032002  
             NIV_SUP := NIV_INF;                                        0000854703033002  
                                                                        0000854803034002  
       END;                                                             0000854903035002  
       NIV_CD:=NIV_SUP + 1;                                             0000855003036002  
     END                                                                0000855103037002  
     ELSE                                                               0000855203038002  
        NIV_CD := NIV_SUP;                                              0000855303039002  
   END                                                                  0000855403040002  
   ELSE                                                                 0000855503041002  
   IF NIVBASE > 0 AND                                                   0000855603042002  
     (IDFFUNCION(ALOG) < 95 OR                                          0000855703042102  
      IDFFUNCION(ALOG) > 95)    THEN                                    0000855803042202  
   BEGIN                                                                0000855903043002  
      REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR : FALTA ARCHIVO ",                       0000856003044002  
                        CDIR[NUM_CDLOG].TITLE        ,                  0000856103045002  
                        ", FAVOR DE COPIARLO   ";                       0000856203046002  
      LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                  0000856303047002  
      ACCEPT(TXTMSG);                                                   0000856403048002  
      GO VERIFICA_CD;                                                   0000856503049002  
   END;                                                                 0000856603050002  
 END OF ARMA_CDIR;                                                      0000856703051002  
  PROCEDURE ACTZA_ARCH;                                                 0000856803052002  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000856903052003  
    DEFINE TFUNCION    = INTEGER(POINTER(TALOG,4),2)#,                  0000857003052004  
           TIDFAUT     = INTEGER((POINTER(TALOG,4)+464),8)#,            0000857103052006  
           TIDFTIPPROC = INTEGER((POINTER(TALOG,4)+488),2)#;            0000857203052008  
                                                                        0000857303052010  
    ARRAY   TALOG[0:59];                                                0000857403052012  
    INTEGER IND_K,FECHA_TEMP,IND_L,IND_PROCT;                           0000857503052014  
    IND_K := -1;                                                        0000857603052042  
    WHILE IND_K := * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                       0000857703052062  
    BEGIN                                                               0000857803052082  
      FECHA_TEMP := ISISDFECARM(ACONTROLE1,IND_K);                      0000857903052102  
      IF FECHA_TEMP GEQ FECPROC THEN                                    0000858003052122  
      BEGIN                                                             0000858103052142  
        REPLACE NOM_ARCMOVT BY ARC_MOV  FOR 34      ,                   0000858203052162  
                             FECHA_TEMP FOR 6 DIGITS,                   0000858303052182  
                             " ON "                 ,                   0000858403052202  
                             PK_MOV     FOR 17,"."  ;                   0000858503052222  
        REPLACE LOGT.TITLE BY NOM_ARCMOVT;                              0000858603052242  
        LOGT.MYUSE := 3;                                                0000858703052262  
        IF LOGT.RESIDENT THEN                                           0000858803052282  
        BEGIN                                                           0000858903052302  
          IND_L := 0;                                                   0000859003052322  
          READ(LOGT[IND_L:=*+1],75,TALOG);                              0000859103052342  
          WHILE TFUNCION NEQ 99 DO                                      0000859203052362  
          BEGIN                                                         0000859303052382  
            IF TFUNCION = 1 THEN                                        0000859403052402  
            BEGIN                                                       0000859503052422  
              IF FEC_X_PROC[IND_K,TIDFTIPPROC] = 0 THEN                 0000859603052442  
                 FEC_X_PROC[IND_K,TIDFTIPPROC] := TIDFAUT;              0000859703052462  
             END;                                                       0000859803052482  
             READ(LOGT[IND_L:=*+1],75,TALOG);                           0000859903052502  
          END;                                                          0000860003052522  
          IND_PROCT := -1;                                              0000860103052542  
          P_FECHAST := POINTER(ARR_PROCESOST);                          0000860203052562  
          WHILE IND_PROCT < 100 DO                                      0000860303052582  
          BEGIN                                                         0000860403052602  
            REPLACE P_FECHAST:P_FECHAST BY                              0000860503052622  
              FEC_X_PROC[IND_K,IND_PROCT] FOR 8 DIGITS;                 0000860603052642  
            IND_PROCT := * + 1;                                         0000860703052662  
          END;                                                          0000860803052682  
          WRITE(PROCESOS[IND_K],150,ARR_PROCESOST[0]);                  0000860903052702  
          REPLACE ARR_PROCESOST[0] BY 48"00"                            0000861003052722  
                                     FOR 150 WORDS;                     0000861103052742  
        END;                                                            0000861203052762  
        IF LOGT.OPEN THEN                                               0000861303052782  
           CLOSE(LOGT);                                                 0000861403052802  
      END;                                                              0000861503052822  
    END;                                                                0000861603052842  
  TERMINA_PROCESOS := TRUE;                                             0000861703052852  
  CLOSE(PROCESOS);                                                      0000861803052857  
  PROCESOS.MYUSE := 3;                                                  0000861903052859  
  END;                                                                  0000862003052862
