 PROCEDURE BAJA;                                                        0000902103359002  
%***************                                                        0000902203360002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000902303361002  
   INTEGER I,J, RESULT_L001;                                            0000902403362002  
                                                                        0000902503363002  
   BOOLEAN BOOL_FIN;                                                    0000902603364002  
   LABEL REGRESA;                                                       0000902703365002  
                                                                        0000902803366002  
%  FINMOV1:= FINMOV2:=FINMOV3:= TRUE;                                   0000902903367002  
   CLOSE (ERRORES1,LOCK);                                               0000903003368002  
   I:= -1;                                                              0000903103369002  
   WHILE I:=*+1 LEQ 9 DO                                                0000903203370002  
         CIERRALOG(I);                                                  0000903303372002  
   REGRESA:                                                             0000903403375002  
   I:= -1;                                                              0000903503377002  
   WHILE I:=*+1 LEQ 9 DO                                                0000903603378002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000903703379002  
      IF STATUS_MIX_P015(I) > 0 THEN                                    0000903803380002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000903903381002  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                             0000904003386002  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY NUM_MIX_P015(I) FOR 7 DIGITS, " HI 4",         0000904103387002  
                           48"00";                                      0000904203388002  
         DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                   0000904303389002  
         WAIT((2));                                                     0000904403389012  
      END;                                                              0000904503391002  
      IF STATUS_MIX_P016(I) > 0 THEN                                    0000904603391102  
      BEGIN                                                             0000904703391202  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                             0000904803391302  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY NUM_MIX_P016(I) FOR 7 DIGITS, " HI 4",         0000904903391402  
                           48"00";                                      0000905003391502  
         DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                   0000905103391602  
         WAIT((2));                                                     0000905203391612  
      END;                                                              0000905303391802  
      IF STATUS_MIX_P025 > 0 THEN                                       0000905403392002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000905503393002  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                             0000905603398002  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY NUM_MIX_P025 FOR 7 DIGITS, " HI 4",            0000905703399002  
                           48"00";                                      0000905803400002  
         DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                   0000905903401002  
         WAIT((2));                                                     0000906003401012  
      END;                                                              0000906103403002  
   END;                                                                 0000906203404002  
   BOOL_FIN := FALSE;                                                   0000906303404022  
   I:= 0            ;                                                   0000906403404032  
   WHILE (NOT BOOL_FIN) AND (I LEQ 9) DO                                0000906503404042  
      IF STATUS_MIX_P015(I) > 0 OR                                      0000906603404052  
         STATUS_MIX_P016(I) > 0 OR                                      0000906703404062  
         STATUS_MIX_P025    > 0 THEN                                    0000906803404072  
            BOOL_FIN := TRUE                                            0000906903404082  
      ELSE                                                              0000907003404092  
         I := * + 1;                                                    0000907103405002  
   IF BOOL_FIN THEN GO REGRESA;                                         0000907203406002  
   REPLACE ACONTROLE2 BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(ACONTROLE2);                   0000907303407002  
   REPLACE DFB05FUNCION(ACONTROLE2) BY    22 FOR 2 DIGITS,              0000907403408002  
                                    SIST_LIB FOR 4 DIGITS,              0000907503409002  
                                    0        FOR 8 DIGITS,              0000907603410002  
                                    2        FOR 4 DIGITS;              0000907703411002  
   RESULT_L001:= B05PROCESOS(ACONTROLE2);                               0000907803412002  
                                                                        0000907903412102  
   IF PROCESOS.OPEN THEN                                                0000908003412202  
      CLOSE(PROCESOS,LOCK);                                             0000908103412302  
                                                                        0000908203428002  
 END OF BAJA;                                                           0000908303429002

 PROCEDURE PROC_CONTROL;                                                0000912203433002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000912303434002  
                                                                        0000912403434082  
%   EXCEPTION PROCEDURE CIERRA_ARCHIVO;                                 0000912503434142  
%   BEGIN                                                               0000912603434202  
%      L := -1;                                                         0000912703434302  
%      WHILE L := * + 1 LEQ 9 DO  CIERRALOG(L);                         0000912803434402  
%      IF LLAMA_MOV   THEN RELINQUISHL(E1);                             0000912903434422  
%      IF LLAMA_SALDO THEN RELINQUISHL(E2);                             0000913003434442  
%   END;                                                                0000913103434502  
   INTEGER EVENTO, NUM_HI, CT_IND;                                      0000913203435002  
   EBCDIC ARRAY DAT_CONTROL[0:11];                                      0000913303436002  
    WHILE MYSELF.LIBRARYUSERS > 0 DO                                    0000913403437002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000913503438002  
      NUM_HI := 0;                                                      0000913603439002  
                                                                        0000913703439302  
      WHILE NUM_HI NEQ 4 DO                                             0000913803440002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000913903441002  
         IF SIST_LIB = 500  THEN                                        0000914003441012  
         BEGIN                                                          0000914103441022  
            IF FIN_S408   AND FIN_S500    AND                           0000914203441032  
               FUNCION_82 AND FUNCION_83  THEN                          0000914303441037  
            BEGIN                                                       0000914403441042  
               EJECUTA_P169     ;                                       0000914503441052  
               FIN_S408 := FALSE;                                       0000914603441062  
               FIN_S500 := FALSE;                                       0000914703441072  
               FUNCION_83 := FUNCION_82 := FALSE;                       0000914803441082  
            END                                                         0000914903441092  
         END;                                                           0000915003441102  
                                                                        0000915103441202  
         EVENTO :=WAITANDRESET((30),MYSELF.EXCEPTIONEVENT,BLOCK_LLENO,  0000915203442002  
                                LEVANTA_PASOS,INICIA_DIA,RESET_EVE,     0000915303442050  
                                AMBIENTA);                              0000915403442052  
         CASE EVENTO OF                                                 0000915503443002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000915603444002  
            1    : BEGIN                                                0000915703444102  
                     IF REG_X_BLOCK_D > 0 OR REG_X_BLOCK_DX > 0 THEN    0000915803445002  
                     BEGIN                                              0000915903445102  
                        CT_IND := -1;                                   0000916003445202  
                        REG_X_BLOCK_D  := 0;                            0000916103445232  
                        REG_X_BLOCK_DX := 0;                            0000916203445262  
                        WHILE CT_IND := * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                  0000916303445302  
                           IF REG_X_BLOCK[CT_IND] > 0 AND               0000916403445332  
                              NIVLOG_A[CT_IND]    > 0 AND               0000916503445402  
                             (FEC_DIA[CT_IND]     = FEC_PRO_BASE  OR    0000916603445502  
                              FEC_DIA[CT_IND]     > FEC_PRO_BASE) THEN  0000916703445602  
                           BEGIN                                        0000916803445652  
                              CIERRALOG(CT_IND);                        0000916903445702  
                              REG_X_BLOCK[CT_IND] := 0;                 0000917003445722  
                           END;                                         0000917103445742  
                     END;                                               0000917203445814  
                   END;                                                 0000917303445902  
            2    : BEGIN                                                0000917403446002  
                     NUM_HI := MYSELF.TASKVALUE;                        0000917503447002  
                     REPLACE DAT_CONTROL[0] BY "0" FOR 12;              0000917603448002  
                     REPLACE DAT_CONTROL[0] BY NUM_HI FOR 4 DIGITS;     0000917703449002  
                     CONTROLES(DAT_CONTROL);                            0000917803450002  
                     RESET(MYSELF.EXCEPTIONEVENT);                      0000917903451002  
                     MYSELF.TASKVALUE := 0;                             0000918003452002  
                  END;                                                  0000918103453002  
            3    : BEGIN                                                0000918203453022  
                     N_BLOCK_LLENO := * + 1;                            0000918303453042  
                     IF N_BLOCK_LLENO = 20   THEN                       0000918403453062  
                       IF REG_X_BLOCK_DX > 0 THEN                       0000918503453082  
                       BEGIN                                            0000918603453102  
                         CT_IND := - 1;                                 0000918703453122  
                         REG_X_BLOCK_DX := 0;                           0000918803453142  
                         WHILE CT_IND := * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                 0000918903453162  
                         BEGIN                                          0000919003453182  
                           IF REG_X_BLOCK[CT_IND] > 0 AND               0000919103453202  
                              NIVLOG_A[CT_IND]    > 0 AND               0000919203453222  
                              FEC_DIA[CT_IND]     > FECPROC THEN        0000919303453242  
                           BEGIN                                        0000919403453262  
                             CIERRALOG(CT_IND);                         0000919503453282  
                             REG_X_BLOCK[CT_IND] := 0;                  0000919603453302  
                           END;                                         0000919703453322  
                         END;                                           0000919803453342  
                         N_BLOCK_LLENO := 0;                            0000919903453362  
                       END;                                             0000920003453382  
                   END;                                                 0000920103453402  
            4    : BEGIN                                                0000920203453422  
%NOR-ISI INICIO SE AGREGA VAL DE STATUS Y EJECUCION DE P015 Y P016      0000920303453442  
                     LEVANTA_P015 := FALSE;                             0000920403453447  
%                    WAIT((3));                                         0000920503453452  
                     IF ACTIVA_P015                    AND              0000920603453462  
                        NIVLOG_A[IND_COPIA]        > 0 AND              0000920703453472  
                        STATUS_MIX_P015(IND_COPIA) < 1 THEN             0000920803453482  
                        BEGIN                                           0000920903453502  
                           EXTERNO_P015;                                0000921003453522  
                           ACTIVA_P015 := FALSE;                        0000921103453542  
                        END;                                            0000921203453562  
                                                                        0000921303453582
